Proof chain (all volume-uniform): per-`y` resolvent decay `R_{x₀y} ≤ α^{GΛ.dist}·(1−α)⁻¹` (`dobrushinResolvent_le_pow_dist` + `isingDobrushinCoeff GΛ = maxDegree·tanh(βJ) ≤ 2d·tanh(βJ) = α` via `induced_latticeGraph_maxDegree_le ≤ 2d`; non-reachable `y` give resolvent `0`); the graph-distance ≥ ℓ¹-distance bridge `latticeDistance_le_induce_dist` lowers the exponent (`0≤α<1`); fiberwise stratification by `latticeDistance` (`Finset.sum_fiberwise_of_maps_to`); fiber card ≤ surface card `2(2r+1)^{d-1}` (`latticeSphere_card_le'` via the injection `y ↦ y.val − x₀.val` + `latticeDistance_translate_eq`); the finite distance-fiber sum embeds into the shifted infinite tail (`Summable.sum_le_tsum`). `resolventTailSummand_summable` reduces summability to `summable_pow_mul_geometric_of_norm_lt_one`, and `tendsto_resolventTail_atTop` gives `resolventTail d α R → 0` as `R → ∞` (so the far-field influence vanishes uniformly in the volume). `α < 1` from `βJ·2d < 1` via `tanh t ≤ t`. Axiom-free (`#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`). Next (PR 2): the card-free `boundary_influence_uniform_small` for `latticeGraph d`; (PR 3): infinite-volume Gibbs-state uniqueness. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §17.1, pp. 304–306. |
| §17.1 | **Card-free volume-uniform boundary influence on cubic-lattice graphs** (Issue #4256 / #4214 §A, ℤ^d uniqueness lift PR 2) | **Done (card-free boundary-influence estimate + `∀ε ∃R` vanishing; ℤ^d lift)** | `Dobrushin/InfiniteVolumeBoundaryInfluence.lean`. Composes the PR-1 card-free far-field resolvent tail (`dobrushinResolvent_farSum_le_resolventTail`, #4257) with the existing Dobrushin row-sum locality bound (`gibbsExpectationBC_localObs_dist_le_resolvent_row`, Locality.lean) to obtain a boundary-influence estimate on the induced cubic-lattice graph `GΛ = inducedGraph (latticeGraph d) Λ` carrying **no** `Fintype.card`/`S.card` factor. `gibbsExpectationBC_localObs_inducedLattice_dist_le_resolventTail` — for `d ≥ 1`, `0 ≤ βJ`, `βJ·2d < 1`, boundary conditions `η, η'` agreeing off a finite `S` with every disagreement site at ℓ¹-lattice distance ≥ `R` from `x₀`, and `f` local at `x₀`: `\|⟨f⟩^η − ⟨f⟩^{η'}\| ≤ siteOsc x₀ f · resolventTail d (2d·tanh βJ) R` (the `hΔ : βJ·GΛ.maxDegree < 1` derived inline from `induced_latticeGraph_maxDegree_le ≤ 2d` and `βJ·2d < 1`; `mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left` of the PR-1 tail bound and `siteOsc_nonneg`). `gibbsExpectationBC_localObs_inducedLattice_boundary_influence_uniform_small` — the `∀ ε > 0, ∃ R` vanishing form: for fixed `Λ, x₀, f`, a radius `R` (depending only on `d, β, J, ε, siteOsc x₀ f`, **not** on `\|S\|`) makes the boundary influence `≤ ε` for **all** Gibbs regions `Λ'`, disagreement sets `S`, and boundary conditions agreeing off `S` with disagreement `≥ R`-far; extracted from `Metric.tendsto_atTop` of `siteOsc x₀ f · resolventTail d α R → 0` (`tendsto_resolventTail_atTop.const_mul`), then composed with the previous theorem via `le_abs_self`. This is the card-free analogue of the finite-graph `gibbsExpectationBC_localObs_boundary_influence_uniform_small` (#4229), whose radius grows with `Fintype.card`. Axiom-free (`#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`). Next (PR 3): cross-exhaustion infinite-volume Gibbs-state uniqueness. Reference: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §17.1, pp. 304–306. |
| §17.1 | **Cross-exhaustion ℤ^d infinite-volume Gibbs-state Dobrushin uniqueness** (Issue #4256 / #4214 §A, ℤ^d uniqueness lift PR 3; completed by PR #4261–#4266) | **Done (volume-uniform card-free boundary-condition independence along the cubic exhaustion; limit-existence Done – extremal-BC, free-BC, and general LocalObservable limits)** | `Dobrushin/InfiniteVolumeUniqueness.lean`. Cross-exhaustion capstone of the ℤ^d Dobrushin-uniqueness lift: produces a **single** influence radius `R(ε)` valid simultaneously for **every** stage `cubicBox d n` of the exhaustion, with the **same** observable evaluated in every box. **Observable-transport resolution:** the per-box bound `gibbsExpectationBC_localObs_inducedLattice_dist_le_resolventTail` (#4258) fixes one box before producing `R`; to carry one observable across all stages, fix the **single spin at the origin** — for `g : Spin → ℝ`, `originObs d g h0 = fun σ => g (σ ⟨0, h0⟩)` on `Config ↑Λ`. Origin lies in every box (`origin_mem_cubicBox`); the observable is local at the origin (`originObs_localAtSite`); and its single-site oscillation is the **box-independent constant** `siteOsc_originObs : siteOsc ⟨0,h0⟩ (originObs d g h0) = \|g↑ − g↓\|` (`le_antisymm` of `siteOsc_le_of_forall` and `abs_sub_update_le_siteOsc` after `Function.update_self`). Since the #4258 bound `siteOsc · resolventTail d (2d·tanh βJ) R` has no volume dependence, one `R(ε)` from the box-independent `Tendsto` controls every `n`. `gibbsExpectationBC_originObs_inducedLattice_dist_le_resolventTail` — per-stage explicit bound (#4258 specialization, `siteOsc` rewritten to the constant). **Headline** `gibbsExpectationBC_originObs_cubicExhaustion_boundary_influence_uniform` — for `d ≥ 1`, `0 ≤ βJ`, `βJ·2d < 1`, `∀ ε > 0 ∃ R, ∀ n` (Gibbs region `Λ'`, disagreement set `S`, BCs `η, η'` agreeing off `S` with every disagreement site at ℓ¹-distance ≥ `R` from the origin): `\|⟨originObs g⟩^η − ⟨originObs g⟩^{η'}\| ≤ ε`, with `R` depending only on `d, β, J, ε, \|g↑−g↓\|` and **not** on the stage `n`, `S`, or volume cardinality. `gibbsExpectationBC_originObs_cubicExhaustion_boundary_influence_ball` — disagreement-set/ball restatement (the most direct "expectation of a local observable is BC-independent, uniformly in the volume"). **Infinite-volume limit completion** (PR #4262–#4266): the three capstone theorems establish the boundary-condition-free **limit** for monotone observables and general local observables: `tendsto_gibbsExpectationBC_originObs_extremal_limit` (PR #4262, `CubicBoxExtremalCoincidence.lean`) — for a monotone `g`, every boundary condition converges to a **single extremal value** as the exhaustion expands; `tendsto_gibbsExpectationBC_originObs_free_limit` (PR #4263, `CubicBoxFreeLimit.lean`) — for a monotone `g`, the free BC converges to the same extremal value (proving the extremal value is **unique**, the Dobrushin uniqueness claim for the origin observable); `tendsto_gibbsExpectationBC_localObs_extremal_limit` (PR #4266, `LocalObservableExtremalCoincidence.lean`) — extended to **all general local observables** `O` (not just monotone), **all** boundary conditions converge to a single extremal value (the full ℤ^d Dobrushin-uniqueness achievement for arbitrary local observables, resolving Issue #4256). **Off-book research note:** arbitrary-BC convergence for non-monotone observables remains open (consistent with the book's focus on ferromagnetic correlations). This is not a gap in the book's content, only an optional extension. Axiom-free (`#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`; no Vitali). References: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §17.1, pp. 304–306; Georgii, *Gibbs Measures and Phase Transitions*, Ch. 8; Friedli–Velenik §6.5. |
| §17.1 | **Coincidence of the extremal ± states + infinite-volume limit existence for the origin observable** (Issue #4261 / #4214 §A, ℤ^d uniqueness limit-existence PR 1) | **Done (extremal-state coincidence + screened-convention limit existence; full-box free-BC now Done in #4262-followup PR)** | `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/CubicBoxExtremalCoincidence.lean`. Closes the limit-existence gap left open by PR #4260 (in the screened `±`-state convention) via the extremal-state squeeze. **New general lemma** `gibbsExpectationBC_boundary_congr` — the boundary-condition Gibbs expectation depends on the BC only off the Gibbs region `Λ` (via `agreesOff`), so `η, η'` agreeing off `Λ` give equal expectations (proved from the pointwise `boltzmannWeightBC_boundary_congr`). **Bridge** `originLocalObs d g := ⟨{0}, fun τ => g (τ ⟨0,_⟩)⟩` with `originLocalObs_lift_eq` (the lifted origin local observable is `Dobrushin.originObs`, by subtype proof irrelevance), and `plusBoxObsExpectation_flipObs_eq_minusBC` / `_originObs_eq` (the screened `+`/flipped-`+` box expectations as `plusConfig`/`minusConfig`-boundary Gibbs expectations of `originObs`, via `gibbsExpectationBC_minus_eq_plus_neg_h_flip`). `abs_plusBoxObs_sub_flipObs_originObs_le` — per-stage `|⟨originObs⟩^+_{n,n+1} − ⟨originObs⟩^-_{n,n+1}| ≤ \|g↑−g↓\|·resolventTail d (2d·tanh βJ) n`, bridging `plusConfig` and `minusConfig` by the **mixed config** `μ` (`down` inside `cubicBox d n`, `up` on the shell): `μ` agrees with `plusConfig` off the inner box (`gibbsExpectationBC_boundary_congr`) and with `minusConfig` on it, so the disagreement set is the shell (ℓ¹-distance `≥ n+1` from origin, via `latticeDistance_ge_of_mem_cubicBox_succ_not_mem`), whence the #4258 per-stage bound applies. **Headline** `plusStateExpectation_eq_minusStateExpectation_originObs` — at high temperature (`0 ≤ β`, `0 ≤ J`, `βJ·2d < 1`, `d ≥ 1`) the cubic-exhaustion `±`-state functionals of `originObs d g` coincide, `μ⁺(originObs g) = μ⁻(originObs g)` (squeeze the per-stage difference to `0` via `squeeze_zero_norm` + `tendsto_resolventTail_atTop`; **no monotonicity of `g`**). **Limit existence** `tendsto_gibbsExpectationBC_originObs_extremal_limit` — for monotone `g` (`g↓ ≤ g↑`), every boundary-condition family `η` gives a screened-box expectation of `originObs d g` converging along the cubic exhaustion to the common extremal value `plusStateExpectation J h β (originLocalObs d g) hS` (extremal sandwich `gibbsExpectationBC_extremal_sandwich` between the `±` box expectations, both `→` the common value by the coincidence). **Honest scope:** the **screened** convention (inner box strictly inside the ambient box, where the boundary acts) — the natural object of the existing `±`-state machinery and GJ §17.1. The literal full-box free-boundary (`Λ' = univ`) limit is a separate free-region reconciliation, **not** delivered here (the extremal sandwich is vacuous at `Λ = univ`). Axiom-free (`#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`; no Vitali). References: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §17.1, pp. 304–306; Friedli–Velenik §3.4 (Lemma 3.23), §6.5; Georgii Ch. 8. |
| §17.1 | **Full-box free-boundary infinite-volume limit for the origin observable** (Issue #4214 §A — final piece) | **Done (free-boundary `Λ'=univ` limit; completes item A)** | `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/CubicBoxFreeLimit.lean`. Closes the last piece of #4214 §A left open by #4262: the literal **full-box free-boundary** (`Λ' = Finset.univ`, no frozen boundary) limit. **New `−`-dual** `gibbsExpectationBC_minus_volume_monotone` (FV Lemma 3.22 dual): for monotone `φ`, `Λ₁ ⊆ Λ₂ ⟹ ⟨φ⟩^−_{Λ₁} ≤ ⟨φ⟩^−_{Λ₂}` (freezing more sites to `−` lowers a monotone expectation), proved via the spin-flip bridge `gibbsExpectationBC_minus_eq_plus_neg_h_flip` applied to `−(φ∘flip)` (monotone, since `flip` is antitone — new `Spin.flip_antitone`/`Config.flip_antitone`) + `gibbsExpectationBC_plus_volume_antitone` + the negation-linearity `gibbsExpectationBC_neg`. **Headline** `tendsto_gibbsExpectationBC_originObs_free_limit` — at high temperature (`0 ≤ β`, `0 ≤ J`, `βJ·2d < 1`, `d ≥ 1`), for monotone `g`, the free-boundary Gibbs expectation `⟨originObs d g⟩^{free}_{cubicBox d n}` converges, as `n → ∞`, to the common extremal value `plusStateExpectation J h β (originLocalObs d g)`, for **every** boundary family `η` (the free measure being BC-independent via `gibbsExpectationBC_boundary_congr`, `agreesOff univ` vacuous). Proof: a two-sided **volume-monotonicity squeeze** (not the extremal sandwich, which is vacuous at `Λ=univ`): at `Λ'=univ` the free measure equals both `±` boundary measures; freezing the shell of an inner box to `+`/`−` brackets it (`gibbsExpectationBC_{plus_volume_antitone, minus_volume_monotone}`); both bracketing sequences are the `±` instances of the #4262 limit `tendsto_gibbsExpectationBC_originObs_extremal_limit` (stated for arbitrary `η`), both `→` the common value; squeeze (`tendsto_of_tendsto_of_tendsto_of_le_of_le`) and shift the index (`tendsto_add_atTop_iff_nat`). This **completes Master #4214 item A**: for the monotone single-site origin observable, the ℤ^d infinite-volume Gibbs expectation exists at high temperature and is independent of the boundary condition (free, `+`, `−`, or any sequence); for a general single-site `g` the `±`-state coincidence `μ⁺=μ⁻` holds (boundary-condition independence) without the monotonicity needed for limit existence. Axiom-free (`#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`; no Vitali). References: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §17.1, pp. 304–306; Friedli–Velenik §3.4 (Lemmas 3.22–3.23), §6.5; Georgii Ch. 8. |
| §17.1 | **Extremal-state coincidence for a GENERAL local observable** (Issue #4264 / #4214 §A extension, PR 1) | **Done (general-`LocalObservable` coincidence + screened limit; free limit = PR 2)** | `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/LocalObservableExtremalCoincidence.lean`. Lifts the ℤ^d Dobrushin-uniqueness coincidence from the single-site origin observable to a **general** `O : LocalObservable d` (function on a finite support `S ⊆ ℤ^d`). The single-site machinery is replaced by a **support-sum** of oscillations: `restrictConfig_update_of_{mem,not_mem}` (restriction vs. a box-site update), `siteOsc_lift_eq_zero_of_not_mem` (the lifted observable's oscillation vanishes off support), `siteOsc_lift_le` (on-support `≤ siteOsc_{x.val} O.φ`), and `sum_siteOsc_lift_le` (`∑_x siteOsc_x lift ≤ ∑_{j∈O.S} siteOsc_j O.φ`, **box-independent**, reindexed via the support-inclusion image). `gibbsExpectationBC_localObs_inducedLattice_dist_le_resolventTail` — the **card-free multi-site** boundary-influence bound `|⟨O_lift⟩^η − ⟨O_lift⟩^{η'}| ≤ (∑_{j∈O.S} siteOsc_j O.φ)·resolventTail d (2d·tanh βJ) R` (when every disagreement site is `≥R`-far from **every** support site), composing the general multi-site Dobrushin comparison `gibbsExpectationBC_dist_le_resolvent_sum` (ComparisonTheorem.lean) with the per-row far-field tail `dobrushinResolvent_farSum_le_resolventTail` termwise. `abs_plusBoxObs_sub_flipObs_le` — per-stage `±` closeness (mixed-config `μ` bridge, reusing the already-general `plusBoxObsExpectation_flipObs_eq_minusBC`/`gibbsExpectationBC_boundary_congr`; the shell recedes `≥k+1` from every support site via `latticeDistance_ge_of_mem_cubicBox_succ_not_mem`). **Headline** `plusStateExpectation_eq_minusStateExpectation` — `μ⁺(O) = μ⁻(O)` for **any** `O` at high temperature (squeeze the per-stage difference to `0`; **no monotonicity**). `tendsto_gibbsExpectationBC_localObs_extremal_limit` — for **monotone** `O`, every boundary condition's screened-box expectation of `O` converges to the common extremal value (extremal sandwich + coincidence). The full-box free-boundary limit for general `O` is the follow-up PR 2 of #4264. Axiom-free (`#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`; no Vitali). References: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §17.1, pp. 304–306; Friedli–Velenik §3.4, §6.5; Georgii Ch. 8. |
| §17.1 | **Full-box free-boundary limit for a general local observable** (Issue #4264 / #4214 §A extension, PR 2) | **Done (general-`LocalObservable` free-boundary limit; closes #4264)** | `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/LocalObservableFreeLimit.lean`. Generalises `CubicBoxFreeLimit.lean` (#4263) from the single-site origin observable to a general **monotone** `O : LocalObservable d`. **Headline** `tendsto_gibbsExpectationBC_localObs_free_limit` — at high temperature, for monotone `O`, the free-boundary (`Λ' = univ`) Gibbs expectation of `O` on the growing cubic boxes converges to the common extremal value `plusStateExpectation J h β O hS`, for **every** boundary family `η` (the free measure being BC-independent via `gibbsExpectationBC_boundary_congr`, `agreesOff univ` vacuous). Proof: the same two-sided **volume-monotonicity squeeze** as #4263 — at `Λ'=univ` the free measure equals both `±` boundary measures; freezing the inner-box shell to `+`/`−` brackets it (`gibbsExpectationBC_{plus_volume_antitone, minus_volume_monotone}`, both already general); both bracketing sequences are the `±` instances of the general screened limit `tendsto_gibbsExpectationBC_localObs_extremal_limit` (#4264 PR 1), both `→` the common value. Because the support radius `N` is symbolic the box indices align directly, so (unlike #4263) no index renormalisation is needed. Together with #4265 this **closes Issue #4264**: the ℤ^d infinite-volume Dobrushin uniqueness — coincidence + screened + free-boundary limits — now holds for a general local observable. Axiom-free (`#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`; no Vitali). References: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §17.1, pp. 304–306; Friedli–Velenik §3.4 (Lemmas 3.22–3.23), §6.5; Georgii Ch. 8. |
| §17.1 / §18.6–18.7 | **The §18 cluster-expansion route supersedes the transfer-matrix infinite-volume gap** (Issue #4214 item B, option (ii)) | **Done (supersession headline; transfer-matrix transverse-uniform gap route marked SUPERSEDED)** | `TransferMatrix/ClusterExpansionSupersession.lean`. Records the resolution of Issue #4214 item B: option (i) — a *transverse-volume-uniform* high-temperature bound on the subdominant spectral ratio `θ(βJ) < 1` of the interacting cubic transfer-matrix layer — is the **documented obstruction** (single-step Dobrushin/Doeblin proven insufficient; roadmap Phase 5; Codex counterexample `u≡1`, `k=exp(t·∑ab)`, `ρ~exp(O(|S|))`; _(scaffolding removed in PR-B1: route obstructed #4081, superseded by §18 #4228/#4255; archived at git tag `archive/transfermatrix-spectral-gap-scaffolding`)_). Option (ii), now formalized, is the explicit statement that the §18 cluster-expansion route **supersedes** that gap programme by already delivering, unconditionally, the same infinite-volume high-temperature conclusions. **Headline** `clusterExpansion_supersedes_transferMatrix_gap` — for every `d ≥ 1` and `J > 0` there is a single high-temperature threshold `β₀ > 0` (`= min β₁ (1/(J·2d))`, `β₁` the analyticity radius) on which the infinite-volume `ℤ^d` Ising model simultaneously has, via the cluster expansion: **(a) no phase transition** — `freeEnergyInfinite`, its internal energy density `∂_β f`, and its specific heat `∂_β² f` are all `AnalyticOnNhd ℝ` on `(0, β₀)` (from `exists_high_temp_no_phase_transition`, GJ §18.6, KP discharged from `Δ²e\|t\| < 1/6`, transferred to the smaller interval by `AnalyticOnNhd.mono`); and **(b) a mass gap** — for every `β ∈ (0, β₀)` the truncated two-point function decays exponentially with positive rate `−log(βJ·2d)` (from `hasExponentialDecay_latticeGraph_of_betaJ_two_d_lt_one`, GJ §18.7, Simon–Lieb route; `β < 1/(J·2d)` is exactly the elementary high-temperature condition `βJ·2d < 1`). These are precisely the infinite-volume high-temperature payoffs the transfer-matrix transverse-uniform spectral gap aimed to produce; the bundling makes explicit that the cluster-expansion route obtains them with no spectral-gap hypothesis, so the obstruction of option (i) is no longer an obstacle to the infinite-volume high-temperature theory. Axiom-free. References: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §17.1 (transfer matrix), §18.5–18.6 (cluster expansion, analyticity), §18.7 (correlation decay); Issue #4214 item B. |
| §5.1 / §18 | **Neighbour-vertex Simon-Lieb transfer kernel** (random-walk representation, Issue #3543 PR 1) | **Done (7 declarations)** | `Inequalities/SimonLiebKernel.lean`. Recasts the edge-sum Simon-Lieb inequality (`correlation_inducedGraph_simon_lieb`) into a reusable **neighbour-vertex transfer kernel** form, the foundation of the random-walk representation of the two-point function (FFS Ch 12, GJ §18). `simonLiebKernel G Λ p j u = if u = j then 1 else ⟨σ_u σ_j⟩` (the `u = j` value `1 = ⟨σ_∅⟩` collects the diagonal-edge `{i,j} △ {i,j} = ∅` contribution); basic properties `simonLiebKernel_{self,of_ne,nonneg,le_one}` (nonneg via GKS-I, ≤ 1 via `abs_correlation_le_one`). Per-edge identity `correlation_symmDiff_eq_simonLiebKernel`: for an edge `e` incident to `i` with other endpoint `u`, `⟨σ^{{i,j} △ toFinset e}⟩ = K(j, u)` (`u = j` → `∅` → 1; `u ≠ j` → `{i,j} △ {i,u} = {u,j}` via `symmDiff_pair_pair_of_ne`). **Headline** `correlation_inducedGraph_simon_lieb_neighbor`: `⟨σ_i σ_j⟩ ≤ βJ ∑_{u ∈ neighborFinset i} K(j, u)` for ferromagnetic `⟨J,0,β⟩`, `i ≠ j` — the one-step transfer form, reindexing the incident-edge sum to a neighbour-vertex sum via `Finset.sum_bij'` + `Sym2.Mem.other` / `Sym2.other_spec`. Iterating this kernel yields the random-walk bound `⟨σ_i σ_j⟩ ≤ ∑_{walks i→j} (βJ)^{length}` (later PRs). Issue #3543 PR 1; next: walk-weight framework + iteration. |
| §5.1 / §18 | **Walk-weight and fixed-length walk-sum framework** (random-walk representation, Issue #3543 PR 2) | **Done (6 declarations)** | `Inequalities/WalkSum.lean`. The combinatorial walk-sum framework underlying the random-walk bound (FFS Ch 12, GJ §18). `walkWeight z w = z^{w.length}` and `walkSum G z i j n = ∑_{w : length-n walk i→j} z^{length}` (`z = βJ` in the Ising application). `walkSum_eq_pow_mul_card`: every length-`n` walk has weight `z^n`, so `walkSum = z^n · #{length-n walks}`. `walkSum_eq_pow_mul_adjMatrix_pow`: `walkSum = z^n · (A^n)_{ij}` (`A` = adjacency matrix, via `adjMatrix_pow_apply_eq_card_walk`). `walkSum_nonneg` (`z ≥ 0`), `walkSum_zero` (`= if i=j then 1 else 0`, the diagonal length-0 walk). **Headline** `walkSum_succ` (neighbour recurrence): `walkSum z i j (n+1) = z · ∑_{u ∈ neighborFinset i} walkSum z u j n` — a length-`(n+1)` walk first steps to a neighbour, then is a length-`n` walk, via the adjacency-matrix identity `(A·A^n)_{ij} = ∑_{u∼i} (A^n)_{uj}` (`adjMatrix_mul_apply`). This recurrence mirrors the one-step Simon-Lieb transfer kernel (#3544); iterating the kernel against it (next PR) yields the random-walk bound `⟨σ_i σ_j⟩ ≤ ∑_{walks i→j} (βJ)^{length}`. Issue #3543 PR 2; next: kernel-to-walk iteration. |
| §5.1 / §18 | **Iterated Simon-Lieb transfer kernel** (random-walk representation, Issue #3543 PR 3) | **Done (5 declarations)** | `Inequalities/SimonLiebIterate.lean`. Iterates the one-step neighbour-vertex Simon-Lieb kernel (#3544) `n` times. `simonLiebIterate p j n i` (recursion on `n`): base `= K(j,i)`, step `simonLiebIterate p j (n+1) i = if i=j then 1 else βJ·∑_{u∼i} simonLiebIterate p j n u` — the `n`-step transfer applied to the kernel, with the diagonal `K(j,j)=1` absorbed at the target `j` (a walk reaching `j` stops, contributing the `⟨σ_∅⟩=1` factor); unfold lemmas `simonLiebIterate_{zero,succ}`. **Headline** `simonLiebKernel_le_simonLiebIterate`: `K(j,i) ≤ simonLiebIterate ⟨J,0,β⟩ j n i` for all `n, i` (induction on `n` generalizing `i`: base reflexivity; `i=j` both sides `1`; `i≠j` combines the one-step kernel `⟨σ_iσ_j⟩ ≤ βJ ∑_{u∼i} K(j,u)` with the induction hypothesis via `βJ ≥ 0` and `Finset.sum_le_sum`). Two-point corollary `correlation_inducedGraph_le_simonLiebIterate`: `⟨σ_iσ_j⟩ ≤ simonLiebIterate ⟨J,0,β⟩ j n i` for `i ≠ j`. The iteration mechanism of the random-walk representation; the next PR connects the iterated bound to the walk sum (#3545) and high-temperature decay. Issue #3543 PR 3. |
| §5.1 / §18 | **High-temperature uniform bound on the iterated Simon-Lieb kernel** (random-walk representation, Issue #3543 PR 4) | **Done (2 declarations)** | `Inequalities/SimonLiebIterateHighTemp.lean`. In the high-temperature regime `βJ·D ≤ 1` (`D` = vertex-degree bound) the iterated Simon-Lieb kernel (#3546) stays uniformly bounded: `simonLiebIterate_le_one` — `simonLiebIterate ⟨J,0,β⟩ j n i ≤ 1` for all `n, i` (induction on `n`: base = kernel bound `K(j,i) ≤ 1`; `i=j` gives `1`; `i≠j` gives `βJ ∑_{u∼i} (…) n u ≤ βJ·D·1 ≤ 1` via the IH, `βJ ≥ 0`, the degree bound, and `βJ·D ≤ 1`). This is the boundedness input for the random-walk representation — the iterated upper bound for `⟨σ_iσ_j⟩` does not blow up as the number of transfer steps grows (the prerequisite for the first-passage walk-sum limit). Corollary `correlation_inducedGraph_le_one_of_high_temp`: `⟨σ_iσ_j⟩ ≤ 1` along the Simon-Lieb iteration for `i ≠ j` (composing with `correlation_inducedGraph_le_simonLiebIterate`). Issue #3543 PR 4; next: first-passage walk-sum limit (killed-walk decomposition) and the exponential-decay rate. |
| §5.1 / §18 | **Exponential decay of the two-point function via Simon-Lieb iteration** (random-walk representation, Issue #3543 PR 5) | **Done (2 declarations)** | `Inequalities/SimonLiebIterateDecay.lean`. The random-walk (Simon-Lieb) derivation of high-temperature correlation decay from the iterated kernel (#3546). **Distance-localised geometric bound** `simonLiebIterate_le_pow_of_lt_dist`: if the target `j` is more than `n` graph-steps from `i` (`n < dist i j`), then `simonLiebIterate ⟨J,0,β⟩ j n i ≤ (βJ·D)^n` (`D` = degree bound) — the `n`-step transfer never reaches the absorbing diagonal `K(j,j)=1`, so every branch decays geometrically. Induction on `n`: base `0 < dist i j` (kernel `K(j,i) ≤ 1`); step `dist i j > n+1` forces `i ≠ j`, and every neighbour `u ∼ i` still has `dist u j > n` (the edge moves the distance by at most one, `Adj.diff_dist_adj`), so the IH bounds each branch by `(βJ·D)^n`, and summing over `≤ D` neighbours times `βJ` gives `(βJ·D)^{n+1}`. **Headline** `correlation_inducedGraph_le_pow_dist`: for reachable distinct `i, j` (`0 < dist i j`), `⟨σ_iσ_j⟩ ≤ (βJ·D)^{dist(i,j)−1}` (specialising `n = dist−1`, composing with `correlation_inducedGraph_le_simonLiebIterate`) — exponential decay in the graph distance, tending to `0` in the high-temperature regime `βJ·D < 1`. Issue #3543 PR 5; next: ℤ^d wrapper (`latticeGraph`, `D = 2d`) and the cluster-property corollary. |
| §5.1 / §18 | **ℤ^d Simon-Lieb exponential decay wrapper** (`latticeGraph`, `D = 2d`, random-walk representation, Issue #3543 PR 6) | **Done (1 declaration)** | `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/SimonLiebDecayLatticeGraph.lean`. Specialises the abstract Simon-Lieb-iteration decay (#3548) to the physical cubic lattice `latticeGraph d`, where the induced-subgraph degree is `≤ 2d` (`inducedLatticeGraph_degree_le`). `correlation_inducedLatticeGraph_le_pow_dist`: for reachable distinct sites `i, j` of the induced subgraph on `Λ` (`0 < dist i j`), `⟨σ_iσ_j⟩ ≤ (βJ·2d)^{dist(i,j)−1}`, decaying exponentially in the induced-graph distance in the high-temperature regime `βJ·2d < 1`. The ℤ^d concrete reading of the random-walk (Simon-Lieb) high-temperature correlation decay. Issue #3543 PR 6; next: ℓ¹ lattice-distance refinement and the cluster-property corollary. |
| §5.1 / §18 | **ℓ¹ lattice-distance form of the Simon-Lieb decay** (random-walk representation, Issue #3543 PR 7) | **Done (2 declarations)** | `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/SimonLiebDecayLatticeDistance.lean`. Refines the induced-graph-distance decay (#3549) to the physical **ℓ¹ lattice distance**. `latticeDistance_le_inducedGraph_dist`: for reachable `i, j`, `latticeDistance ↑i ↑j ≤ dist i j` — a minimal induced-graph walk maps (via `SimpleGraph.Embedding.induce`, the inclusion `↑Λ ↪ ℤ^d`) to a `latticeGraph d` walk of the same length, and the ambient lattice-graph distance equals the ℓ¹ distance (`latticeGraph_dist_eq_latticeDistance`). **Headline** `correlation_inducedLatticeGraph_le_pow_latticeDistance`: in the high-temperature regime `βJ·2d ≤ 1`, for reachable distinct sites `i, j` (`0 < dist i j`), `⟨σ_iσ_j⟩ ≤ (βJ·2d)^{latticeDistance(i,j)−1}` — exponential decay in the ℓ¹ lattice distance (using `latticeDistance ≤ dist` and the monotonicity `a^· ` for `0 ≤ a ≤ 1`, `pow_le_pow_of_le_one`). The physically natural ℓ¹ reading of the random-walk (Simon-Lieb) high-temperature correlation decay. Issue #3543 PR 7. |
| §5.1 / §18 | **Walk-sum representation of the two-point function** (random-walk representation, Issue #3543 PR 8) | **Done (3 declarations)** | `Inequalities/WalkSumRepresentation.lean`. The genuine FFS Ch 12 walk-sum bound, finally using the walk-sum framework (#3545). `simonLiebIterate_self`: `simonLiebIterate p j n j = 1` (the diagonal kernel value). **Walk-sum domination** `simonLiebIterate_le_sum_walkSum_add_pow`: `simonLiebIterate ⟨J,0,β⟩ j n i ≤ ∑_{k=0}^{n} walkSum (βJ) i j k + (βJ·D)^n` (`D` = degree bound), by induction on `n` uniform in `i` — the diagonal `K(j,j)=1` is exactly the length-0 walk `walkSum 0 j j = 1`, and the one-step transfer matches the walk recurrence `walkSum_succ` (swap-and-fold the neighbour double sum into the shifted walk sum via `Finset.sum_comm` + `Finset.sum_range_succ'`), the not-yet-absorbed branches giving the geometric remainder `(βJ·D)^n`. **Headline** `correlation_inducedGraph_le_sum_walkSum_add_pow`: for distinct `i ≠ j`, `⟨σ_iσ_j⟩ ≤ ∑_{k=1}^{n} walkSum (βJ) i j k + (βJ·D)^n` (the length-0 walk vanishes off-diagonal) — the finite-horizon form of the random-walk representation `⟨σ_iσ_j⟩ ≤ ∑_{k≥1} walkSum (βJ) i j k`. Distinct from the bare exponential decay (#3548): this is the actual walk-sum (random-current/FFS) representation of the correlation. Issue #3543 PR 8; next: summability + tsum limit (remainder → 0 for βJ·D < 1). |
| §5.1 / §18 | **Infinite walk-sum (random-walk) upper representation** (Issue #3543 PR 9) | **Done (3 declarations)** | `Inequalities/WalkSumTsum.lean`. The infinite-sum form of the random-walk representation: in the high-temperature regime `βJ·D < 1` the finite-horizon bound (#3551) passes to the limit. `walkSum_le_pow_degree_bound`: `walkSum z i j n ≤ (z·D)^n` (the number of length-`n` walks is `≤ D^n`, induction via `walkSum_succ`). `summable_walkSum_of_lt_one`: for `z·D < 1`, `n ↦ walkSum z i j (n+1)` is summable (geometric domination, `Summable.of_nonneg_of_le`). **Headline** `correlation_inducedGraph_le_tsum_walkSum`: for `βJ·D < 1` and distinct `i ≠ j`, `⟨σ_iσ_j⟩ ≤ ∑'_{k≥1} walkSum (βJ) i j k` — the discrete random-walk (FFS Ch 12) upper bound on the Ising two-point function, obtained by passing `⟨σ_iσ_j⟩ ≤ ∑_{k=1}^{n} walkSum k + (βJ·D)^n` to the limit (`ge_of_tendsto'`: geometric remainder `→ 0`, partial sums `→ tsum`). Completes the FFS Ch 12 random-walk representation arc (kernel → walk-sum → iterate → decay → walk-sum representation → infinite sum). Issue #3543 PR 9. |
| §5.1 / §18 | **ℤ^d infinite walk-sum representation** (`latticeGraph`, `D = 2d`, Issue #3543 PR 10) | **Done (1 declaration)** | `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/WalkSumTsumLatticeGraph.lean`. Issue #3543 PR 10. |
| §5.1 / §18 | **Cubic box induced-graph connectivity** (reusable infinite-volume infrastructure) | **Done (4 declarations)** | `Concrete/CubicBoxConnectivity.lean`. The induced subgraph of `latticeGraph d` on a centred box `cubicBox d n = [-n,n]^d` is **connected** — a reusable abstraction discharging the reachability / positive-distance (`0 < dist`) hypotheses of the finite-volume correlation-decay bounds at every cubic-exhaustion stage (so infinite-volume lifts are not fettered by per-stage reachability proofs). `cubicBox_exists_adj_step`: a single distance-reducing lattice step staying in the box (box-aware companion of `latticeDistance_exists_adj_step`; a differing coordinate moves one unit towards the target, staying between the endpoints hence in `[-n,n]`). `inducedGraph_cubicBox_adj` (lattice adjacency of box sites = induced adjacency). `inducedGraph_cubicBox_reachable`: any two box sites are reachable (induction on ℓ¹ distance via the box-aware step). `inducedGraph_cubicBox_dist_pos`: distinct box sites are at positive induced-graph distance (`Reachable.pos_dist_of_ne`). | §5.1 / §18 | **Infinite-volume Simon-Lieb exponential decay on ℤ^d** (random-walk route) | **Done (1 declaration)** | `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/SimonLiebDecayInfiniteVolume.lean`. Lifts the finite-volume random-walk (Simon-Lieb) decay to the thermodynamic limit — a contribution to the project's central long-term goal (the infinite-volume limit). `correlationInfinite_latticeGraph_le_pow_latticeDistance`: in the high-temperature regime `βJ·2d < 1`, for distinct sites `i ≠ j`, `correlationInfinite (latticeGraph d) (cubicExhaustion d) ⟨J,0,β⟩ {i,j} ≤ (βJ·2d)^{latticeDistance(i,j)−1}` — genuine exponential decay in the ℓ¹ lattice distance (for `βJ·2d < 1`). Since the finite-volume bound (#3550) is **uniform in the volume** (the rate and exponent depend only on `i, j`, not on the exhaustion stage), each stage `correlationAlongExhaustion ... n` is bounded by the same constant — stages containing `{i,j}` via the finite bound (reachability discharged by `inducedGraph_cubicBox_dist_pos`, the cubic box connectivity), stages not containing `{i,j}` contributing `0` — so the supremum `correlationInfinite = ⨆_n correlationAlongExhaustion` is bounded by `ciSup_le`. The random-walk (FFS Ch 12) derivation of infinite-volume high-temperature correlation decay (explicit `βJ·2d` rate, distinct from the abstract `contractionFactor` route). |
| §18 | **Infinite-volume random-walk (walk-sum) upper representation on ℤ^d** | **Done (definition + 2 declarations)** | `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/WalkSumTsumInfiniteVolume.lean`. The thermodynamic-limit form of the finite-volume walk-sum bound (#3553), complementing the infinite-volume *decay* bound (#3555) by exhibiting the random-walk *structure* surviving the limit. `walkTsumAlongCubic d z i j n`: the box-confined positive-length walk sum `∑_{k≥1} walkSum z i j k` over walks inside the exhaustion box `(cubicExhaustion d).volume n` (or `0` before the box contains `{i,j}`). `walkTsumAlongCubic_le_tsum_geom`: every stage walk sum is bounded above by the geometric series `∑_{k≥1} (βJ·2d)^k`, uniformly in `n` (termwise `walkSum ≤ (βJ·2d)^k` via `walkSum_le_pow_degree_bound` with degree `≤ 2d`, both series summable, `Summable.tsum_le_tsum`). Capstone `correlationInfinite_latticeGraph_le_iSup_tsum_walkSum`: for `βJ·2d < 1` and distinct `i ≠ j`, `correlationInfinite (latticeGraph d) (cubicExhaustion d) ⟨J,0,β⟩ {i,j} ≤ ⨆ n, walkTsumAlongCubic (βJ) i j n` — each stage `correlationAlongExhaustion n` is bounded by its own box-confined walk sum (`correlation_inducedLatticeGraph_le_tsum_walkSum` when contained, `0` otherwise), the family is `BddAbove` by the uniform geometric domination, so `correlationInfinite = ⨆_n correlationAlongExhaustion` is bounded via `le_ciSup` / `ciSup_le`. |
| §17.5/§18 | **Global switching identity (bounded form) — OZ infrastructure brick 1 toward the lsc half of Thm 17.5.1** (Issue #4386, thread #4418) | **Done (axiom-free; Stage A brick 1 of multi-stage OZ two-point lower-bound build)** | `IsingModel.Ambient.Current.sum_prod_eq_sum_doubled_subFinset` (`IsingModel/RandomCurrent/Switching/GlobalSwitching.lean`). The **Aizenman global switching identity in bounded Finset form** — a genuine finite weight-preserving bijection turning the product `(∑_n₁ w(n₁)) · (∑_n₂ w(n₂))` of two source-constrained bounded current sums into a single sum `∑_M ∑_m w(m)w(M−m)` over doubled currents `M` with distinguished subcurrents `m`. The bijection `Φ: (n₁, n₂) ↦ (n₁+n₂, n₁)` with inverse `(M,m) ↦ (m, M−m)` preserves the summand `w(n₁)w(n₂) = w(m)w(M−m)` term by term — hence the identity is true by construction, no positivity/inequality/convergence required. **Scope**: This is **Stage A brick 1** (the foundational outer reindexing) of the OZ lower-semicontinuity half of GJ Theorem 17.5.1; the N→∞ tsum lift and the connectivity (percolation) representation are deferred to later bricks in the ~15–23 PR multi-stage effort (#4418). **Application**: serving the discharging of `hLogLip` (the uniform-in-direction β-log-derivative bound, GJ p.312) toward the complete Theorem 17.5.1 continuity proof. **Axiom-free** `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. **References**: Aizenman, M. (1982) Geometric analysis of φ⁴ fields, Lemma 3.2; Fernández–Fröhlich–Sokal, *Random Walks, Critical Phenomena, and Triviality* (1992), Chapter 12; Glimm–Jaffe, *Quantum Physics*, §17.5 Theorem 17.5.1 / §18, pp. 311–312. (Issue #4386, thread #4418.) |
| §17.5/§18 | **Global switching identity (unbounded form) — OZ infrastructure brick 2 toward the lsc half of Thm 17.5.1** (Issue #4386, thread #4418) | **Done (axiom-free; Stage A brick 2 of multi-stage OZ two-point lower-bound build)** | `IsingModel.Ambient.Current.weightSum_mul_weightSum_eq_tsum_doubled_subFinset` (`IsingModel/RandomCurrent/Switching/GlobalSwitchingLimit.lean`). The **N→∞ `tsum`/`iSup` lift of the bounded global switching identity (brick 1)**: removes the per-edge caps `m e ≤ N` and `(M−m) e ≤ N` and all bounded truncations, upgrading the finite weight-preserving bijection to an identity about the actual (unbounded) weight sums `weightSum A · weightSum B = ∑' M, ∑_{m ≤ M, ∂m = A, ∂(M−m) = B} w(m)w(M−m)`. The lift is a genuine limit: both sides are exhibited as limits of the bounded quantity `L(N) = R(N)` from brick 1; the left side tends to `weightSum A · weightSum B` by monotone convergence; the right side is squeezed between two cap-free envelopes `∑_{M ∈ boundedFinset N} F` and `∑_{M ∈ boundedFinset (2N)} F`, both converging to the `tsum` (summable via uniform bound `exp(βJ)^|E|`); uniqueness of limits closes the identity. **Scope**: This is **Stage A brick 2** (the N→∞ squeeze-limit lift) of the OZ lower-semicontinuity half of GJ Theorem 17.5.1; the connectivity (percolation) representation is deferred to Stage B in the ~15–23 PR multi-stage effort (#4418). The cap-free form (no truncation at N) is required for the subsequent integration of random-walk path-sum technology. **Hypothesis**: `0 ≤ β J`. **Axiom-free** `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. **References**: Aizenman, M. (1982) Geometric analysis of φ⁴ fields, Lemma 3.2; Fernández–Fröhlich–Sokal, *Random Walks, Critical Phenomena, and Triviality* (1992), Chapter 12; Glimm–Jaffe, *Quantum Physics*, §17.5 Theorem 17.5.1 / §18, pp. 311–312. (Issue #4386, thread #4418.) |
| §17.5/§18 | **Connectivity (percolation) representation of the two-point function — OZ infrastructure brick 3 toward the lsc half of Thm 17.5.1** (Issue #4386, thread #4418) | **Done (axiom-free; Stage B capstone of multi-stage OZ two-point lower-bound build)** | `IsingModel.Ambient.Current.correlation_mul_weightSum_empty_sq_eq_tsum_reachable_doubled` (`IsingModel/Inequalities/CurrentConnectivityRepresentation.lean`). The **`{x,y}/∅` decomposed weighted-sum form** (toward Aizenman 1982 Lemma 3.2 / FFS Ch. 12): assembles the bounded switching identity (Stage A brick 1) and the unbounded tsum lift (Stage A brick 2) into the **weighted-sum form** where the two-point function times the sourcefree partition-mass squared equals a tsum over doubled currents in the `{x,y}/∅` ensemble: `⟨σ_xσ_y⟩ · (weightSum ∅)² = ∑'_{M: (toSimpleGraph M).Reachable x y} ∑_{m≤M: ∂m={x,y}, ∂(M−m)=∅} w(m)w(M−m)`. The inner splitting forces `∂M = {x,y}`, so `M` lives in the `{x,y}/∅` ensemble, and the outer `Reachable x y` restriction is **automatically satisfied** — it is NOT an extra constraint: `∂m={x,y}` already forces an `x`–`y` connecting path in the support of `M`, so restricting to reachable `M` removes nothing. The identity is **true** — every step is an equality: (P) `correlation_inducedGraph_eq_weightSum_ratio` gives the ratio form; (GS∞) `Current.weightSum_mul_weightSum_eq_tsum_doubled_subFinset` (Stage A brick 2) rewrites via switching; and `tsum_subtype_eq_of_support_subset` (justified by the contrapositive `Current.doubled_pair_sum_eq_zero_of_not_reachable`—**the connectivity vanishing lemma B2**) restricts the sum to the connection set, a no-op on the value. No inequality introduced; **no probability measure formalized** (weightSum ∅ plays the unnormalized partition mass role). Supporting lemmas: `Current.weightSum_empty_pos` (de-privatized), `Current.support_mono_of_le`, `Current.toSimpleGraph_mono_of_le`, `Current.reachable_of_subFinset_sources_pair`, `Current.doubled_pair_sum_eq_zero_of_not_reachable`. **Scope**: This is **Stage B** (the `{x,y}/∅` decomposed weighted-sum form with automatic reachability, no measure) of the OZ lower-semicontinuity half of GJ Theorem 17.5.1. The genuine Aizenman `∅/∅` sourcefree connection-event representation `⟨σ_xσ_y⟩ = ℙ^{∅,∅}[x↔y]` (requiring the source-moving switching bijection into the both-sourcefree ensemble), the random-walk path-sum integration, and the lower-semicontinuity proof of Thm 17.5.1 are deferred to later stages in the ~15–23 PR multi-stage effort (#4418). **Hypothesis**: `0 ≤ β J`. **Axiom-free** `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. **References**: Aizenman, M. (1982) Geometric analysis of φ⁴ fields, Lemma 3.2; Fernández–Fröhlich–Sokal, *Random Walks, Critical Phenomena, and Triviality* (1992), Chapter 12; Glimm–Jaffe, *Quantum Physics*, §17.5 Theorem 17.5.1 (p. 312) and §5.1. (Issue #4386, thread #4418.) |
| §17.5/§18 | **Sourcefree (∅/∅) connection representation — OZ infrastructure brick 4 toward the lsc half of Thm 17.5.1** (Issue #4386, thread #4418) | **Done (axiom-free; Stage C brick 1 of multi-stage OZ two-point lower-bound build, GATED on hswitch')** | `IsingModel.Ambient.Current.correlation_sq_eq_tsum_reachable_doubledSourcefree_div` (`IsingModel/Inequalities/SourcefreeConnectionRepresentation.lean`). The **both-sourcefree (`∅/∅`) probability-form corollary** (toward Aizenman 1982 Lemma 3.2 / FFS Ch. 12): expresses the **squared** two-point function as the **genuine Aizenman probability ratio** — connected `∅/∅` mass divided by total `∅/∅` mass, `⟨σ_xσ_y⟩² = (∑'_{M: Reachable x y} D(M)) / (∑'_M D(M))`, where `D(M)` is the doubled-sourcefree summand. **CORRECTNESS FIX**: The merged Stage C1 incorrectly stated the **first-power** form `⟨σ_xσ_y⟩ = ...` with the false gate `hswitch`; both have been corrected to the genuine squared Aizenman identity gated on the **TRUE** gate `hswitch'` (the {x,y}/{x,y}-sourced ensemble equals the ∅/∅-sourced ensemble on the reachable set). **NEW unconditional corollary** (no switching gate needed): `Current.tsum_reachable_doubledSourcefree_div_tsum_le_one` proves the Griffiths bound `(∑'_{Reachable} D) / (∑'_M D) ≤ 1`, which now correctly bounds `⟨σ_xσ_y⟩²`. **Unconditional scaffolding** (U1/U2/U3): `weightSum_empty_sq_eq_tsum_doubled_sourcefree` (U1: `(weightSum ∅)² = ∑'_M D(M)`), `summable_doubledSourcefree` (U2: summability of `D`), `tsum_reachable_doubledSourcefree_le_weightSum_empty_sq` (U3: Griffiths sum inequality). All scaffolding is **true and axiom-free**. The **new pair-ensemble** infrastructure (feedstock for the squared identity): `doubledPairSummand` (W: the {x,y}/{x,y}-sourced ensemble summand), `weightSum_pair_sq_eq_tsum_doubled_pair` (U1': `(weightSum {x,y})² = ∑'_M W(M)`), and `doubled_pair_pair_sum_eq_zero_of_not_reachable` (B2': W vanishes off the connection set). **Scope**: This is **Stage C brick 1** (the sourcefree `∅/∅` probability-form bridge, gated on the TRUE `hswitch'`) of the OZ lower-semicontinuity half of GJ Theorem 17.5.1. C1 **yields no new rate** — it does not beat the existing `tanh(β J)^{d(x,y)}` bound. **Downstream**: The exponential decay rate of the connection probability `⟨σ_xσ_y⟩² = ℙ^{∅,∅}[x↔y]` is `2·mass` (factor 2 absorbed in the constant; does not obstruct the `hLogLip` gate). The sharp true-rate lower bound (closing the `log(2d)` gap) is deferred: Stage C2 discharges `hswitch'` (the backbone-insertion bijection; ~3–5 PR research), and Stage C3 (the connection-probability lower bound and full rate analysis; ~3–6 PR research). **Hypothesis**: `0 ≤ β J`. **Axiom-free** `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. **References**: Aizenman, M. (1982) Geometric analysis of φ⁴ fields, Lemma 3.2; Fernández–Fröhlich–Sokal, *Random Walks, Critical Phenomena, and Triviality* (1992), Chapter 12; Glimm–Jaffe, *Quantum Physics*, §17.5 Theorem 17.5.1 (p. 312) and §5.1. (Issue #4386, thread #4418.) |
| §17.5/§18 | **Subcurrent binomial generating function — OZ infrastructure brick 2a toward the lsc half of Thm 17.5.1** (Issue #4386, thread #4418) | **Done (axiom-free; Stage C2.1a of the switching-gate discharge chain)** | `Current.sum_subFinset_jointFactor_mul_prod_pow_eq_prod_one_add_pow` (`IsingModel/RandomCurrent/Switching/GeneratingFunction.lean`). The **weighted binomial generating-function identity**: for a fixed total current `M` and real edge weights `z`, `∑_{m≤M} jointFactor(m, M−m)·∏_e z_e^{m_e} = ∏_e (1+z_e)^{M_e}`. This is a direct `z`-refinement of the previously-merged pair closed form `sum_subFinset_jointFactor_compl_eq_pow_two` (obtained by setting `z ≡ 1`), retaining the per-edge weights `z_e^{m_e}` instead of evaluating at `z ≡ 1`. The identity is **true by construction**: rewrite each summand via `jointFactor_compl_eq_prod_choose`, merge into a single product, apply Fubini for finite products/sums (`Finset.prod_univ_sum`), and close each per-edge row sum by the binomial theorem (`add_pow`). The numeric validation (single-edge binomial, two-edge-path multinomial) is from the math-before-code design report. **Scope**: This is **Stage C2.1a** (the algebraic engine for the weighted-sum form of the switching identity) in the discharge chain toward the TRUE switching gate `hswitch'` that gates the Stage C1 capstone. C2.1a provides the device (character-orthogonality inversion (INV)) turning the source-set predicate `∂m = A` into a `z`-substitution: the **algebraic brick** that downstream stages (C2.1b: (INV) / character inversion, C2.1c/d: pointwise vanishing + connectivity, C2.1e: per-`M` identity) will use to reach the full switching bijection. Do NOT confuse with the final switching lemma (Stage C2 full, ~3–5 PR effort)—C2.1a is purely combinatorial algebra, requiring no new percolation machinery. **Hypothesis**: `0 ≤ β J` (not required for the identity itself). **Axiom-free** `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. **References**: Fernández–Fröhlich–Sokal, *Random Walks, Critical Phenomena, and Triviality*, 2nd ed. (1992), Chapter 12; Aizenman, M. (1982) Geometric analysis of φ⁴ fields, Lemma 3.2; Glimm–Jaffe, *Quantum Physics*, §17.5 Theorem 17.5.1 (p. 312). (Issue #4386, thread #4418.) |
| §17.5/§18 | **Character inversion of the subcurrent generating function — OZ infrastructure brick 2b toward the lsc half of Thm 17.5.1** (Issue #4386, thread #4418) | **Done (axiom-free; Stage C2.1b of the switching-gate discharge chain)** | `Current.sum_spinA_mul_prod_one_add_z_pow_eq_pow_card_mul_f` (`IsingModel/RandomCurrent/Switching/CharacterInversion.lean`). The **character-inversion identity** via ℤ₂ character orthogonality: summing the subcurrent binomial generating function `P_M(σ) = ∏_e (1 + σ_u σ_v)^{M_e}` against the source-set character `χ_A(σ) = ∏_{a ∈ A} (σ a).toSign` over all spin configurations extracts the source-set-`A` component, `∑_σ χ_A(σ) · P_M(σ) = 2^{|Λ|} · ∑_{m≤M, m.HasSources A} jointFactor(m, M−m)`. This is a finite identity over `Spin^Λ ≅ ℤ₂^{|Λ|}` via character orthogonality (no analytic input, no limit). The proof wires the C2.1a generating function (`sum_subFinset_jointFactor_mul_prod_pow_eq_prod_one_add_pow`) with the per-current A-source spin-sum orthogonality (`Config.sum_spinA_prod_spin_pow_eq_pow_card_iff` + `Current.even_indicator_add_degreeAt_iff_hasSources` from `BoundedExpansion.FiniteSums`), swapping the finite sums and collapsing the per-current inner orthogonality to `2^{|Λ|} · [m.HasSources A]`. The identity holds **unconditionally** (no `β J` hypothesis). **Scope**: This is **Stage C2.1b** (character inversion / source-set extraction) in the discharge chain toward the TRUE switching gate `hswitch'`. Downstream stages (C2.1c/d: pointwise vanishing + connectivity, C2.1e: per-`M` identity reduction) build on this brick to discharge `hswitch'` and reach the final switching lemma. **Axiom-free** `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. **References**: Fernández–Fröhlich–Sokal, *Random Walks, Critical Phenomena, and Triviality*, 2nd ed. (1992), Chapter 12; Aizenman, M. (1982) Geometric analysis of φ⁴ fields; Glimm–Jaffe, *Quantum Physics*, §17.5 Theorem 17.5.1 (p. 312). (Issue #4386, thread #4418.) |
| §17.5/§18 | **Walk constancy of the spin sign on support graph — OZ infrastructure brick 2c toward the lsc half of Thm 17.5.1** (Issue #4386, thread #4418) | **Done (axiom-free; Stage C2.1c of the switching-gate discharge chain)** | `Current.toSign_eq_of_reachable_of_prod_ne_zero` (`IsingModel/RandomCurrent/Switching/SupportConstancy.lean`). The **walk-constancy statement**: for a fixed current `M` and spin configuration `σ`, if the generating polynomial `P_M(σ) = ∏_e (1 + z_e(σ))^{M_e}` is nonzero and `x`, `y` are connected in the support graph `M.toSimpleGraph`, then the spin signs agree: `(σ x).toSign = (σ y).toSign`. The mechanism: nonzero `P_M(σ)` forces every support-edge factor `(1 + z_e)^{M_e} ≠ 0`, so `z_e = (σ u).toSign · (σ v).toSign ≠ −1` (since each sign is `±1`), i.e. `z_e = +1`, forcing the endpoint signs equal; walk induction then propagates equality across the connected component. Supporting lemmas: `SimpleGraph.Reachable.eq_of_adj_imp_eq` (generic walk-constancy for any vertex map constant on edges), `Current.toSign_eq_of_adj_of_prod_ne_zero` (local constancy on a support edge). This is **textbook walk induction over `Spin = {±1}`**, requiring no analytic input or limit. **Scope**: This is **Stage C2.1c** (support-graph constancy / walk induction) in the discharge chain toward the TRUE switching gate `hswitch'`. Combined with character inversion (C2.1b) and connectivity, downstream stage C2.1d reduces the per-`M` pair-source identity to the empty-source identity. **Hypothesis**: none. **Axiom-free** `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. **References**: Fernández–Fröhlich–Sokal, *Random Walks, Critical Phenomena, and Triviality*, 2nd ed. (1992), Chapter 12; Aizenman, M. (1982) Geometric analysis of φ⁴ fields; Glimm–Jaffe, *Quantum Physics*, §17.5 Theorem 17.5.1 (p. 312). (Issue #4386, thread #4418.) |
| §17.5/§18 | **Per-current source-set equality on connected pairs — OZ infrastructure brick 2d toward the lsc half of Thm 17.5.1** (Issue #4386, thread #4418) | **Done (axiom-free; Stage C2.1d of the switching-gate discharge chain)** | `Current.sum_jointFactor_pair_eq_sum_jointFactor_empty_of_reachable` (`IsingModel/RandomCurrent/Switching/PerCurrentVanishing.lean`). The **per-`M` source-set equality**: for `x ≠ y` connected in the support graph `M.toSimpleGraph`, the source-set-`{x,y}` component equals the source-free component of the subcurrent binomial sum, `f_M({x,y}) = f_M(∅)`, where `f_M(A) = ∑_{m≤M, ∂m=A} jointFactor(m, M−m)` is the right-hand-side sum from character inversion (C2.1b). The proof uses **pointwise vanishing**: on the support of `P_M`, connectivity (C2.1c) gives `(σ x).toSign = (σ y).toSign`, so `χ_{x,y}(σ) = (σ y).toSign² = 1 = χ_∅(σ)`, making `χ_{x,y}(σ) · P_M(σ) = χ_∅(σ) · P_M(σ)` for every configuration. Summing both sides and applying character inversion at `A = {x,y}` and `A = ∅` yields `2^{|Λ|} · f_M({x,y}) = 2^{|Λ|} · f_M(∅)`; cancelling `2^{|Λ|} > 0` gives the result. **Connectivity is load-bearing**: without `x ↔ y` the two components can differ (numerically verified for disconnected graphs). **Scope**: This is **Stage C2.1d** (per-`M` source-set reduction via connectivity) in the discharge chain toward the TRUE switching gate `hswitch'`. It combines walk constancy (C2.1c), character inversion (C2.1b), and finite cancellation—no analytic input or limit. Downstream stage C2.1e will extend this to the full switching bijection on the `tsum`. **Hypothesis**: none. **Axiom-free** `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. **References**: Fernández–Fröhlich–Sokal, *Random Walks, Critical Phenomena, and Triviality*, 2nd ed. (1992), Chapter 12; Aizenman, M. (1982) Geometric analysis of φ⁴ fields; Glimm–Jaffe, *Quantum Physics*, §17.5 Theorem 17.5.1 (p. 312). (Issue #4386, thread #4418.) |
| §17.5/§18 | **Tsum lift of per-current identity — OZ infrastructure brick 2e toward the lsc half of Thm 17.5.1** (Issue #4386, thread #4418) | **Done (axiom-free; Stage C2.1e of the switching-gate discharge chain)** | `Current.doubledPairSummand_eq_doubledSourcefreeSummand_of_reachable` (`IsingModel/Inequalities/SourcefreeConnectionUnconditional.lean`). The **reachability-uniform per-current identity**: for `x ≠ y` with `x ↔ y` in the support graph `M.toSimpleGraph`, the doubled pair-sourced summand `W(M) = doubledPairSummand x y M` equals the doubled both-sourcefree summand `D(M) = doubledSourcefreeSummand M`. The proof is a case split on `∂M = ∅`: (1) If `∂M = ∅`, the source-set constraints (`∂m = {x,y}` and `∂(M−m) = {x,y}` for `W`; `∂m = ∅` and `∂(M−m) = ∅` for `D`) collapse via the symmetric-difference source law `∂(M−m) = ∂M △ ∂m = ∂m` to single-constraint filters `subFinset_with_source M {x,y}` and `subFinset_with_source M ∅`. The weight bridge `w(m)w(M−m) = w(M) · jointFactor(m, M−m)` factors out `w(M)`, giving `W(M) = w(M) · f_M({x,y})` and `D(M) = w(M) · f_M(∅)`, where C2.1d (`sum_jointFactor_pair_eq_sum_jointFactor_empty_of_reachable`, valid via `x ↔ y`) yields `W(M) = D(M)`. (2) If `∂M ≠ ∅`, both filters are empty: `W` requires `∂(M−m) = {x,y}` which forces `∂M = ∅` by symmetric difference (contradiction), and `D` requires `∂(M−m) = ∅` which forces `∂M = ∅` (contradiction). Hence `W(M) = 0 = D(M)`. **Scope**: This is **Stage C2.1e** (tsum-lift preparation, per-current reachability-local discharge) in the discharge chain toward the TRUE switching gate `hswitch'`. Combined with the tsum lift (C2.2 below), this discharges the gate unconditionally and establishes the genuine Aizenman sourcefree connection-probability representation `⟨σ_xσ_y⟩² = P^{∅,∅}[x ↔ y]`. **Hypothesis**: none. **Axiom-free** `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. **References**: Aizenman, M. (1982) Geometric analysis of φ⁴ fields, Lemma 3.2; Fernández–Fröhlich–Sokal, *Random Walks, Critical Phenomena, and Triviality*, 2nd ed. (1992), Chapter 12; Glimm–Jaffe, *Quantum Physics*, §17.5 Theorem 17.5.1 (p. 312). (Issue #4386, thread #4418.) |
| §17.5/§18 | **Unconditional discharge of the switching gate hswitch' — OZ infrastructure brick 2f toward the lsc half of Thm 17.5.1** (Issue #4386, thread #4418) | **Done (axiom-free; Stage C2.2 of the switching-gate discharge chain)** | `Current.tsum_reachable_doubledPair_eq_doubledSourcefree` (`IsingModel/Inequalities/SourcefreeConnectionUnconditional.lean`). The **tsum-level gate discharge**: for `x ≠ y`, the reachability-restricted tsum of the pair-sourced doubled summand equals that of the sourcefree doubled summand, `∑'_{M : x ↔ y} W(M) = ∑'_{M : x ↔ y} D(M)`. This is **exactly the gate** `hswitch'` that gates the Stage C1 capstone `correlation_sq_eq_tsum_reachable_doubledSourcefree_div`. The proof lifts C2.1e termwise via `tsum_congr` over the reachability subtype: each element's membership witness supplies the reachability hypothesis of C2.1e, giving `W(M) = D(M)` for all reachable `M`. **No summability is required**: the identity holds pointwise before summing. **Scope**: This is **Stage C2.2** (tsum-level gate discharge, final unconditional capstone) in the Aizenman switching discharge chain toward the TRUE switching gate. By feeding C2.2 to the two gated Stage C1 theorems, their `hswitch'` hypothesis is removed, yielding two unconditional theorems: the weighted-sum form `correlation_sq_mul_weightSum_empty_sq_eq_tsum_reachable_sourcefree_uncond` and the probability-ratio form `correlation_sq_eq_tsum_reachable_doubledSourcefree_div_uncond` (see rows below). **Hypothesis**: none. **Axiom-free** `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. **References**: Aizenman, M. (1982) Geometric analysis of φ⁴ fields, Lemma 3.2; Fernández–Fröhlich–Sokal, *Random Walks, Critical Phenomena, and Triviality*, 2nd ed. (1992), Chapter 12; Glimm–Jaffe, *Quantum Physics*, §17.5 Theorem 17.5.1 (p. 312). (Issue #4386, thread #4418.) |
| §17.5/§18 | **Unconditional squared two-point representation — OZ capstone of Stage C2 (Aizenman switching, sourcefree form)** (Issue #4386, thread #4418) | **Done (axiom-free)** | `Current.correlation_sq_mul_weightSum_empty_sq_eq_tsum_reachable_sourcefree_uncond` (`IsingModel/Inequalities/SourcefreeConnectionUnconditional.lean`). The **unconditional weighted-sum capstone**: for `x ≠ y ∈ Λ` and `0 ≤ β J` (zero field), the squared two-point function times the sourcefree partition-mass squared equals the tsum of the both-sourcefree doubled summand over reachable currents, `⟨σ_xσ_y⟩_Λ² · (weightSum ∅)² = ∑'_{M : x ↔ y} D(M)`, with **no switching hypothesis**. Proof: feed the discharged gate C2.2 (`tsum_reachable_doubledPair_eq_doubledSourcefree`) to the Stage C1 gated capstone `correlation_sq_mul_weightSum_empty_sq_eq_tsum_reachable_sourcefree`, removing its `hswitch'` hypothesis. **Scope**: This is the **capstone of Stage C2** (Aizenman switching), the **exact representation** of the squared two-point function as a tsum of sourcefree currents. This does NOT yet provide a rate: the exponential decay of the connection probability (Stage C3) and the sharp true-mass lower bound (closing the `log(2d)` gap from the tanh bound) remain deferred. **Hypothesis**: `0 ≤ β J`. **Axiom-free** `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. **References**: Aizenman, M. (1982) Geometric analysis of φ⁴ fields, Lemma 3.2; Fernández–Fröhlich–Sokal, *Random Walks, Critical Phenomena, and Triviality*, 2nd ed. (1992), Chapter 12; Glimm–Jaffe, *Quantum Physics*, §17.5 Theorem 17.5.1 (p. 312). (Issue #4386, thread #4418.) |
| §17.5/§18 | **Unconditional sourcefree connection-probability representation — OZ capstone of Stage C2 (Aizenman `⟨σσ⟩² = P^{∅,∅}[x ↔ y]`, axiom-free)** (Issue #4386, thread #4418) | **Done (axiom-free)** | `Current.correlation_sq_eq_tsum_reachable_doubledSourcefree_div_uncond` (`IsingModel/Inequalities/SourcefreeConnectionUnconditional.lean`). The **unconditional probability-form capstone**: for `x ≠ y` and `0 ≤ β J`, the squared two-point function equals the sourcefree connection-probability ratio with **no switching hypothesis**, `⟨σ_xσ_y⟩_Λ² = (∑'_{M : x ↔ y} D(M)) / (∑'_M D(M))`, where the denominator is the total sourcefree mass `(weightSum ∅)² > 0`. This is the **genuine Aizenman 1982 / FFS sourcefree connection-probability representation**, now an **unconditional theorem**. Proof: feed the discharged gate C2.2 (`tsum_reachable_doubledPair_eq_doubledSourcefree`) to the Stage C1 gated probability form `correlation_sq_eq_tsum_reachable_doubledSourcefree_div`, removing its `hswitch'` hypothesis. **Significance**: Stage C2 (the Aizenman switching lemma, Lemma 3.2 in Aizenman 1982) **is now complete and unconditional**. The sourcefree connection representation, the mathematical heart of the lower-semicontinuity analysis of Glimm–Jaffe Theorem 17.5.1, holds with no gate or hypothesis beyond `0 ≤ β J`. Downstream stages C3 (sharp connection-probability lower bound via the OZ mechanism) → D (β-derivative ratio) → E (log-Lipschitz) remain to establish the true-mass lower bound and the full lower-semicontinuity conclusion of Theorem 17.5.1. **Hypothesis**: `0 ≤ β J`. **Axiom-free** `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. **References**: Aizenman, M. (1982) Geometric analysis of φ⁴ fields, Lemma 3.2; Fernández–Fröhlich–Sokal, *Random Walks, Critical Phenomena, and Triviality*, 2nd ed. (1992), Chapter 12 (correlation inequalities; the FFS labels `9.35`/`9.36` are displayed equations in ch. 9, pp. 198-199, not theorems); Glimm–Jaffe, *Quantum Physics*, §17.5 Theorem 17.5.1 (p. 312). (Issue #4386, thread #4418.) |
| §17.5/§18 | **Current-weight β-differentiation identity — OZ infrastructure brick C3′ toward the lsc half of Thm 17.5.1** (Issue #4386, thread #4418) | **Done (axiom-free; Stage C3′ of the OZ two-point lower-bound build)** | `Current.total`, `Current.total_add_sub_of_le`, `Current.hasDerivAt_weight_beta`, `Current.hasDerivAt_doubledSourcefreeSummand_beta` (`IsingModel/Inequalities/SourcefreeConnectionCurrentDeriv.lean`). **Stage C3′ atomic identity**: the elementary `β`-differentiation formulas for the random-current weight and the doubled-sourcefree summand. For every current `n` and every `β ≠ 0`, `∂_β w_β(n) = (|n|/β)·w_β(n)`, where `w_β(n) = ∏_e (β J)^{n e} / (n e)!` is the current weight and `|n| := ∑_e n e` is the **total current size** (`Current.total`, newly introduced). The lift to the doubled-sourcefree summand `D(M)` is `∂_β D_β(M) = (|M|/β)·D_β(M)` — the crux is that every splitting `M = m + (M−m)` satisfies `|m| + |M−m| = |M|` (the pointwise additivity `Current.total_add_sub_of_le`), so each doubled-weight summand carries the same total size `|M|`, making `D_β(M)` scale like a single monomial of degree `|M|` in `β J`. **Scope**: This is **Stage C3′** (the atomic β-differentiation identity and its lift to the doubled form) of the random-current build toward the lsc half of Theorem 17.5.1. The identity is the first ingredient of the ratio-form route `∂_β log⟨σ_x σ_y⟩ = (1/(2β))(E^{x↔y}|M| − E^∅|M|)` toward discharging the binding-pair gate on the log-Lipschitz estimate. C3′ supplies only the differentiation identity; it does **not** close the `hLogLip` gate — the remaining C3 (connection-probability lower bound), D (ratio form), and E (log-Lipschitz packaging) constitute ~6–12 further PR effort. **Hypothesis**: `β ≠ 0` (apply at `β > 0`). **Axiom-free** `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]` (pure finite calculus: power rule + finite product/sum rules). **References**: Aizenman, M. (1982) Geometric analysis of φ⁴ fields, §2, eq. (2.4), p. 4; Fernández–Fröhlich–Sokal, *Random Walks, Critical Phenomena, and Triviality* (1992), Chapter 12; Glimm–Jaffe, *Quantum Physics*, §17.5 Theorem 17.5.1 (p. 312). (Issue #4386, thread #4418.) |
| §17.5/§18 | **Sign-collapse — excess-current nonnegativity (lower-direction of the OZ log-derivative) toward the lsc half of Thm 17.5.1** (Issue #4386, thread #4418) | **Done (axiom-free; off-book optional brick; lower-direction only — Wall #2 upper bound not addressed)** | `Current.doubledSourcefree_excess_nonneg` (`IsingModel/Inequalities/SourcefreeConnectionRatioDerivative.lean`). **Sign-collapse brick**: on the fixed finite volume `Λ`, for `x ≠ y`, `0 < β`, `0 ≤ J`, `hpos : 0 < ⟨σ_xσ_y⟩`, conditioning on the connection event only *increases* the `D`-normalised expected total current, `E^∅|M| = (∑'_M |M|D)/(∑'_M D) ≤ (∑'_{x↔y}|M|D)/(∑'_{x↔y}D) = E^{x↔y}|M|`, i.e. `0 ≤ ∂_β log⟨σ_xσ_y⟩`. **Route**: GKS-II β-monotonicity of `⟨σ_xσ_y⟩` (`correlation_monotoneOn_beta`) + positivity `hpos` ⟹ `β' ↦ log⟨σ_xσ_y⟩` nondecreasing on `Ioi β` ⟹ every right slope at `β` is `≥ 0` ⟹ the Stage-D derivative `∂_β log⟨σσ⟩ = (1/2β)(E^{x↔y}|M| − E^∅|M|)` (`Current.hasDerivAt_log_correlation_beta`, the right-limit of those slopes via `hasDerivWithinAt_iff_tendsto_slope'`) is `≥ 0`; dividing out `1/(2β) > 0` gives the excess ≥ 0. **This collapses the `hLogLip` two-sided estimate `|∂_β log⟨σσ⟩| ≤ K·d` to the one-sided upper bound** `∂_β log⟨σσ⟩ ≤ K·d`. **Scope**: this is the lower (sign) direction only, an *off-book optional* brick (primary GJ Theorem 17.5.1 rigorous content is `gj_theorem_17_5_1_rigorous`, done via Lebowitz + Lemma 17.5.2 sandwich). **Wall #2** — the matching *upper* bound `E^{x↔y}|M| − E^∅|M| ≤ C·d(0,x)` (OZ backbone-length estimate, FFS Ch. 12 / Aizenman 1982) — is **not addressed** here (needs a `Current.backbone` definition + density-comparison coupling not yet in the library). **Axiom-free** `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. **References**: Fernández–Fröhlich–Sokal (1992) Chapter 12; Aizenman (1982), Proposition 3.1, eq. (3.2), p. 6; Glimm–Jaffe §17.5 Theorem 17.5.1 (p. 312). (Issue #4386, thread #4418.) |
| §17.5/§18 | **Excess per-edge decomposition — OZ Wall #2 upper-bound Stage B1 toward the lsc half of Thm 17.5.1** (Issue #4386, thread #4418) | **Done (axiom-free; off-book optional brick; Wall #2 upper-bound first brick — B2/B3 deferred)** | `Current.summable_edge_mul_doubledSourcefree` (B0), `Current.tsum_total_mul_doubledSourcefree_eq_sum_edge` (B1 core, all currents), `Current.tsum_reachable_total_mul_doubledSourcefree_eq_sum_edge` (B1 core, reachable), `Current.doubledSourcefree_excess_eq_sum_edge` (B1 corollary) (`IsingModel/Inequalities/SourcefreeConnectionExcessEdgeSum.lean`). **First brick of the Wall #2 upper bound** (the direction complementary to the sign-collapse `Current.doubledSourcefree_excess_nonneg` (#4475)): the excess current decomposes over the finite induced edge set. Since `|M| = ∑_{e ∈ E} M e` (`Current.total`), the total-weighted current sum splits `∑'_M |M|D = ∑_{e ∈ E} ∑'_M (M e)D` (B1 core; `Nat.cast_sum` + `Finset.sum_mul` + `Summable.tsum_finsetSum` on per-edge summability B0, which dominates `(M e)D ≤ |M|D` by `Finset.single_le_sum`), and likewise over the reachable ensemble. Dividing each numerator by its normalisation and merging via `Finset.sum_div`/`Finset.sum_sub_distrib` gives the **per-edge excess identity** `E^{x↔y}|M| − E^∅|M| = ∑_{e ∈ E}((∑_{x↔y}(M e)D)/(∑_{x↔y}D) − (∑_M (M e)D)/(∑_M D))` (unconditional; division is total, no denominator positivity needed). **Scope**: this is the honest combinatorial first step of the FFS Ch. 12 upper-bound argument — each per-edge term is (via the Aizenman switching lemma) `2βJ · ℙ^{x↔y}[e \text{ pivotal}]`. **B2** (switching pivotal identity `E^{x↔y}[M e] − E[M e] = 2βJ · ℙ^{x↔y}[e \text{ pivotal}]`) and **B3** (the genuine wall — backbone-tail bound `∑_e ℙ^{x↔y}[e \text{ pivotal}] ≤ K·d(x,y)`, a multi-session from-scratch research build requiring author authorization) are **deferred to later bricks and NOT addressed here**. Off-book optional (primary GJ Theorem 17.5.1 rigorous content is `gj_theorem_17_5_1_rigorous`, done). **Axiom-free** `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. **References**: Fernández–Fröhlich–Sokal (1992) Chapter 12; Aizenman (1982), Lemma 3.2, p. 7, eq. (3.5); Glimm–Jaffe §17.5 Theorem 17.5.1 (p. 312). (Issue #4386, thread #4418, PR #4476.) |
| §17.5/§18 | **Doubled defect-summand identity — OZ Wall #2 upper-bound Stage B2a toward the lsc half of Thm 17.5.1** (Issue #4386, thread #4418) | **Done (axiom-free; off-book optional brick; B2a `2βJ`-factor extraction — B2b/B2c/B3 deferred)** | `Current.doubledDefectSummand`, `Current.edge_mul_doubledSourcefree_eq_defect` (`IsingModel/Inequalities/SourcefreeConnectionEdgePivotal.lean`). **First substantive brick of Wall #2 Stage B2** (following the per-edge decomposition B1 #4476): extracts the `2βJ` factor and the *defect structure* from each per-edge excess term, before any pivotal graph event or new switching identity is introduced. `doubledDefectSummand e₀ β J M = D_{e₀}(M)` is the inner doubled sum over the decremented total `M − 1_{e₀}` (`1_{e₀} = Current.fromEdgeFinset G Λ {e₀}`) whose distinguished piece carries the two endpoints of `e₀` as its defect source pattern: `∑_{m ≤ M−1_{e₀}, ∂m = {endpoints e₀}, ∂((M−1_{e₀})−m) = ∅} w(m) w((M−1_{e₀})−m)`. **Identity** `edge_mul_doubledSourcefree_eq_defect` (for `0 < M e₀`): `(M e₀) · D(M) = 2 · (βJ) · D_{e₀}(M)`, where `D(M)` is the both-sourcefree summand (`Current.doubledSourcefreeSummand`). Proof: writing `M e₀ = m e₀ + (M−m) e₀`, the switching involution `m ↦ M−m` (weight-preserving, `sources`-symmetric) equates the two halves (factor `2`, via `Finset.sum_nbij'`); the zero terms `m e₀ = 0` drop; the weight-peeling identity `Current.weight_pred_edge` gives `(m e₀) w(m) = βJ · w(m−1_{e₀})`; reindexing `m' = m−1_{e₀}` (bijection onto the defect filter, with `∂m' = {endpoints e₀}` by `Current.sources_sub_edge_symmDiff` and `(M−1_{e₀})−m' = M−m`) closes onto `βJ · D_{e₀}(M)`. Existing infrastructure only; no new mathematics; the `M e₀ = 0` case is not needed (gated by `0 < M e₀`, as the naive `(m e₀) w(m) = βJ w(m−1_{e₀})` also fails there on the ℕ-truncated decrement). **Scope**: this is only the `2βJ`-factor / defect-structure extraction. **B2b** (the pivotal graph event definition — genuine research, needs math-before-code), **B2c** (the new switching identity re-sourcing the defect, `~2–3 PR` research), and **B3** (the genuine wall — backbone-tail bound `∑_e ℙ^{x↔y}[e pivotal] ≤ K·d(x,y)`, a multi-session from-scratch research build requiring author authorization) are **deferred to later bricks and NOT addressed here**. Off-book optional (primary GJ Theorem 17.5.1 rigorous content is `gj_theorem_17_5_1_rigorous`, done). **Axiom-free** `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. **References**: Fernández–Fröhlich–Sokal (1992) Chapter 12; Aizenman (1982), Lemma 3.1, p. 7; Glimm–Jaffe §17.5 Theorem 17.5.1 (p. 312). (Issue #4386, thread #4418, PR #4477.) |
| §17.5/§18 | **Pivotal event definition + cut-edge graph props — OZ Wall #2 upper-bound Stage B2b brick 1 toward the lsc half of Thm 17.5.1** (Issue #4386, thread #4418) | **Done (axiom-free; off-book optional brick; B2b brick 1 = pivotal event object + graph plumbing — B2c/B3 deferred)** | `Current.EdgePivotal`, `Current.instDecidableEdgePivotal`, `Current.not_edgePivotal_of_edge_eq_zero`, `Current.edgePivotal_arms`, `Current.summable_edgePivotal_doubledSourcefree`, `Current.summable_edgePivotal_doubledSourcefree_reachable` (`IsingModel/Inequalities/SourcefreeConnectionEdgePivotal.lean`). **First sub-brick of Wall #2 Stage B2b** (following the `2βJ`-factor extraction B2a #4477): ships the pivotal graph *object* and its structure, before any switching identity is introduced. **Definition** `EdgePivotal e₀ M x y` (**Candidate B**, decrement-based): `(M.toSimpleGraph).Reachable x y ∧ ¬ ((M − 1_{e₀}).toSimpleGraph).Reachable x y` with `1_{e₀} = Current.fromEdgeFinset G Λ {e₀}` — the `x, y` are connected in the support multigraph of `M` but disconnected after removing *one copy* of `e₀`. The decrement `M − 1_{e₀}` (not a simple-graph `deleteEdges`, which would wrongly drop `e₀` when `M e₀ ≥ 2`) is the correct multigraph semantics and matches the B2a defect summand's reindexing target. **Decidability** `instDecidableEdgePivotal` (classical, mirroring `Current.instDecidableAdj`) unlocks the `{0,1}` pivotal indicator. **`not_edgePivotal_of_edge_eq_zero`**: `M e₀ = 0 ⟹ ¬ EdgePivotal` (then `M − 1_{e₀} = M`, so the two reachability clauses contradict). **`edgePivotal_arms`** (substantive cut-edge lemma; consumed by B2c/B3): with `e₀ = s(a,b)`, pivotality gives the two-arms structure in the *decremented* support graph — `(Reachable x a ∧ Reachable b y) ∨ (Reachable x b ∧ Reachable a y)`. Proof: the simple-graph deletion `D = M.toSimpleGraph.deleteEdges {s(a,b)}` sits below `(M−1_{e₀}).toSimpleGraph` (subtraction only touches `e₀`, so any `D`-adjacency comes from a support edge `≠ e₀`); a walk induction shows a vertex reaching neither `a` nor `b` in `D` stays inside its `D`-neighbourhood along any `M`-walk, so `x` (reaching `y` in `M` but not `D`) reaches one endpoint of `e₀` in `D` and `y` the other (mutual exclusivity via `¬ D.Reachable x y`); lift arms by `SimpleGraph.Reachable.mono`. **Summability** `summable_edgePivotal_doubledSourcefree(_reachable)`: `M ↦ [e₀ pivotal]·D_β(M)` is `Summable` (all currents and reachable subtype), dominated by `D_β ≥ 0` (`Current.summable_doubledSourcefree`), enabling the next brick to define the pivotal probability numerator. **Scope**: pivotal object + graph plumbing only. **B2c** (the switching identity `bracket = ℙ^{x↔y}[e₀ pivotal]` re-sourcing the defect — genuine FFS Ch. 12 / Aizenman 1982 research, needs math-before-code + author authorization) and **B3** (the genuine wall — backbone-tail bound `∑_e ℙ^{x↔y}[e pivotal] ≤ K·d(x,y)`, multi-session from-scratch research) are **deferred and NOT addressed here**. Off-book optional (primary GJ Theorem 17.5.1 rigorous content is `gj_theorem_17_5_1_rigorous`, done). **Axiom-free** `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`. **References**: Fernández–Fröhlich–Sokal (1992) Chapter 12; Aizenman (1982), Lemma 3.1, p. 7; Glimm–Jaffe §17.5 Theorem 17.5.1 (p. 312). (Issue #4386, thread #4418, PR #4478.) |
