| §17.5 | Lattice Chebyshev (ℓ∞) hyperplane distance — GJ §17.5 `a₀ = d` input | **Done (definition + 4 lemmas)** | `latticeDistanceInf (d : ℕ) (x y : Fin d → ℤ) : ℕ := Finset.univ.sup (fun i => (x i − y i).natAbs)` (`Lattice.lean`), the ℓ∞ (Chebyshev) maximum-coordinate distance = the lattice **hyperplane separation** of Glimm–Jaffe §17.5 (p. 312), in which the transfer matrix gives exponential decay `e^{−m·dist}` of correlations. Lemmas: `latticeDistanceInf_self` (`= 0`), `latticeDistanceInf_comm` (symmetry), `latticeDistanceInf_eq_zero_iff` (`= 0 ↔ x = y`), `latticeDistanceInf_triangle` (triangle inequality), `latticeDistanceInf_pos_of_ne` (`> 0` for `x ≠ y`) — completing the ℓ∞ pseudo-metric structure — together with `latticeDistanceInf_le_latticeDistance` (`max ≤ sum`, ℓ∞ ≤ ℓ¹) and `latticeDistance_le_card_mul_latticeDistanceInf` (`|x−y|₁ ≤ d·|x−y|_∞`, `sum ≤ card·max`) — the geometric input `dist ≥ |x−y|/a₀` with `a₀ = d`, converting transfer-matrix decay in the hyperplane separation into ℓ¹-distance decay at rate `m/d`. **Rate conversion** (`Lemma_17_5_2/ChebyshevRateBridge.lean`): `rate_div_card_mul_latticeDistance_le` (`(m/d)·|x−y|₁ ≤ m·|x−y|_∞` for `m ≥ 0`), `exp_neg_rate_latticeDistanceInf_le` (`e^{−m·|x−y|_∞} ≤ e^{−(m/d)·|x−y|₁}`), and `le_exp_neg_rate_latticeDistance_of_le_exp_neg_latticeDistanceInf` (a transfer-matrix bound `c ≤ A·e^{−m·|x−y|_∞}` transfers to `c ≤ A·e^{−(m/d)·|x−y|₁}`) — exactly how GJ §17.5 turns transfer-matrix decay in the hyperplane separation into the `e^{−m·dist}` bound. **Hyperplane decay → `HasExponentialDecay` bridge** (`Lemma_17_5_2/HasExponentialDecayChebyshev.lean`): `HasExponentialDecay_of_latticeDistanceInf_bound` — a hyperplane-separation (ℓ∞) decay hypothesis on the ∞-volume truncated two-point function, `|S^T_2(i,j)| ≤ A·e^{−m·|i−j|_∞}` for all `i ≠ j`, implies the project's ℓ¹ `Ambient.HasExponentialDecay d Λ p (m/d)` at the reduced rate `m/d`. This lets a future transfer-matrix hyperplane decay estimate feed directly into the `latticeMass` machinery without constructing the transfer-matrix spectral operator. Part of the GJ §17.5 Lemma 17.5.2 sharp transfer-matrix programme (Issue #1645). |
| §5.1 | Distance-based cluster decay capstone (compose PR #779 + PR #782) | **Done (conditional, distance-based form)** | `comap_latticeDistance_atTop_eq_cofinite` (`Lattice.lean`): `Filter.comap (fun j => latticeDistance d i j) Filter.atTop = Filter.cofinite`. The `≥` direction is `Filter.Tendsto.le_comap` of PR #782's `tendsto_latticeDistance_atTop_cofinite`; the `≤` direction uses `Set.Finite.bddAbove` on `ℕ` to bound `latticeDistance d i` on the finite complement of any cofinite set, then takes `Set.Ici (M+1) ∈ Filter.atTop`. ℤ^d capstone `truncated2Infinite_latticeGraph_tendsto_atTop_zero_of_summable` (`Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/CorrelationDecay.lean`): under summability of `j ↦ U_2(i, j)`, `Tendsto (fun j => truncated2Infinite (latticeGraph d) Λ p i j) (Filter.comap (fun j => latticeDistance d i j) Filter.atTop) (nhds 0)`. Equivalent ε-N reading: for every `ε > 0` there exists `N : ℕ` such that `latticeDistance d i j ≥ N` implies `|U_2(i, j)| < ε`. The proof is a one-line rewrite of the comap filter via the equality lemma followed by PR #779's cofinite version. The capstone is a `Summable`-conditioned corollary, not a standalone GJ result: it presents the §5.1 cluster picture in its distance-based form, with the `Summable` hypothesis serving as the conditional input later discharged in high-temperature regimes by the Simon–Lieb stack (Simon 1980; Lieb 1980). Together with PRs #779 / #781 / #782 this completes the conditional §5.1 cluster-decay infrastructure stack. (PR #783.) |
| §5.1 / §17.4 | **UNCONDITIONAL high-temperature distance cluster decay** (Issue #4274, discharges the `Summable` hypothesis) | **Done (unconditional at `βJ·2d < 1`)** | `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/CorrelationDecay.lean`. Discharges the `Summable` placeholder of the conditional capstone above at high temperature, giving the **unconditional** ℤ^d cluster-decay statement. `truncated2Infinite_latticeGraph_summable_of_high_temp` — for any exhaustion `Λ`, ferromagnetic `⟨J,0,β⟩` (`0≤J`, `0<β`), and `β·J·2d < 1`, the infinite-volume Ursell two-point function `j ↦ U₂(i,j)` is `Summable`, via `truncated2Infinite_summable_of_high_temp` (finite susceptibility bound `βJ·2d/(1−βJ·2d)`, FV §3.7.3 / Simon–Lieb GKS-II) with the per-vertex incident-edge count `≤ 2d` (`edgeFilter_card_eq_degree` + `inducedLatticeGraph_degree_le`, exactly as in `clusterProperty_latticeGraph_of_high_temp`). `truncated2Infinite_latticeGraph_tendsto_atTop_zero_of_high_temp` and `…_tendsto_cofinite_zero_of_high_temp` — the unconditional distance-`→∞` (and `cofinite`) decay `U₂(i,j) → 0`, composing the summability into the conditional wrappers. This resolves the gap audited as #4267 B7 (the one tractable item of the audit). Axiom-free (`#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`; no Vitali — the chain is the GKS/Simon–Lieb finite-volume layer only). References: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §5.1 pp. 72–74; Friedli–Velenik §3.7.3 (Simon–Lieb / susceptibility). |
| §5.1 | `LocallyFinite (latticeGraph d)` + ambient degree ≤ 2d (Simon–Lieb prep) | **Done (ambient `Fintype` instance + degree bound)** | `latticeGraph_neighborSet_fintype` + `latticeGraph_degree_le` (`Concrete/LatticeGraphBED.lean`). Provides `Fintype ((latticeGraph d).neighborSet v)` for every `v : Fin d → ℤ`, i.e. `LocallyFinite (latticeGraph d)`, via `Fintype.ofFinset` on `(latticeNeighborEnum d v).filter ((latticeGraph d).Adj v)` together with `latticeGraph_adj_mem_neighborEnum`. The degree bound `(latticeGraph d).degree v ≤ 2 * d` follows from `Finset.card_le_card` of the inclusion `neighborFinset v ⊆ latticeNeighborEnum d v` and the existing `latticeNeighborEnum_card_le`. Until now the project's `Fintype` reasoning all happened on the induced subgraph `(inducedGraph (latticeGraph d) Λ)` for finite `Λ`; this PR is the first time the unrestricted ambient `latticeGraph d` is given a `LocallyFinite` instance, unlocking the standard `neighborFinset` / `degree` API and making the next infrastructure layer toward the Simon–Lieb inequality of Simon 1980 / Lieb 1980 (outer vertex boundary, edge boundary, etc.) statable in mathlib's standard idiom. (PR #784.) |
| §5.1 | Outer vertex boundary `∂_o^v S` of a Finset (Simon–Lieb prep step 2) | **Done (generic def + 4 lemmas + ℤ^d card bound)** | `SimpleGraph.outerVertexBoundary G S := S.biUnion G.neighborFinset \ S` (placed in mathlib's `SimpleGraph` namespace, `Concrete/LatticeGraphBED.lean`) for any `[LocallyFinite G] [DecidableEq V]`. Lemmas: `mem_outerVertexBoundary_iff` (`y ∈ ∂_o^v S ↔ y ∉ S ∧ ∃ x ∈ S, G.Adj x y`), `outerVertexBoundary_disjoint` (`Disjoint (∂_o^v S) S`), `outerVertexBoundary_empty` (`∂_o^v ∅ = ∅`), `outerVertexBoundary_card_le_sum_degrees` (`|∂_o^v S| ≤ ∑ x ∈ S, G.degree x` via `Finset.sdiff_subset` + `Finset.card_biUnion_le` + `card_neighborFinset_eq_degree`). ℤ^d wrapper `IsingModel.Ambient.latticeGraph_outerVertexBoundary_card_le`: `((latticeGraph d).outerVertexBoundary S).card ≤ 2 * d * S.card`, combining the generic sum-of-degrees bound with PR #784's `latticeGraph_degree_le`. Together with PR #784 this exposes the standard outer-vertex-boundary indexing object as preparation for the eventual Simon–Lieb statement (Simon 1980; Lieb 1980; Epic #780 B-iii). (PR #785.) |
| §5.1 | Inner vertex boundary `∂_i^v S` + outer-by-inner degree bound (Simon–Lieb prep step 3) | **Done (generic def + 3 lemmas + outer-by-inner bound + ℤ^d wrapper)** | `SimpleGraph.innerVertexBoundary G S := S.filter (fun x => ¬ (G.neighborFinset x ⊆ S))` (mathlib's `SimpleGraph` namespace, `Concrete/LatticeGraphBED.lean`) for `[LocallyFinite G] [DecidableEq V]`. Lemmas: `mem_innerVertexBoundary_iff` (`x ∈ ∂_i^v S ↔ x ∈ S ∧ ∃ y, G.Adj x y ∧ y ∉ S` via `Finset.not_subset` + `mem_neighborFinset`), `innerVertexBoundary_subset_self` (`⊆ S` via `Finset.filter_subset`), `innerVertexBoundary_empty` (`∂_i^v ∅ = ∅`). Substantive outer-by-inner boundary degree bound `outerVertexBoundary_card_le_sum_degrees_innerVertexBoundary`: `(∂_o^v S).card ≤ ∑ x ∈ ∂_i^v S, G.degree x` — every outer-boundary vertex `y` is reached as a neighbour of some inner-boundary vertex `x` (the witness for `y ∈ ∂_o^v S` has the outside neighbour `y`, hence sits in `∂_i^v S`); `outerVertexBoundary G S ⊆ (innerVertexBoundary G S).biUnion neighborFinset`, then `Finset.card_biUnion_le` + `card_neighborFinset_eq_degree`. The outer boundary is upper-bounded by a sum of degrees ranging only over the inner boundary. ℤ^d wrapper `latticeGraph_outerVertexBoundary_card_le_two_mul_d_mul_innerVertexBoundary_card`: `((latticeGraph d).∂_o^v S).card ≤ 2d · ((latticeGraph d).∂_i^v S).card` — the elementary max-degree-based linear bound on `ℤ^d` (not the optimal vertex-isoperimetric inequality). (PR #786.) |
| §5.1 | Oriented edge boundary `∂^e S` of a Finset (Simon–Lieb prep step 4) | **Done (generic def + 3 lemmas + ℤ^d card bound)** | `SimpleGraph.edgeBoundary G S : Finset (V × V)` := `(innerVertexBoundary G S).biUnion (fun x => ((neighborFinset x).filter (· ∉ S)).image (fun y => (x, y)))` (mathlib's `SimpleGraph` namespace, `Concrete/LatticeGraphBED.lean`) for `[LocallyFinite G] [DecidableEq V]`. Each crossing edge is recorded exactly once with its `S`-endpoint listed first, sidestepping the `Sym2 V` quotient handling. Lemmas: `mem_edgeBoundary_iff` (`(x, y) ∈ ∂^e S ↔ x ∈ S ∧ y ∉ S ∧ G.Adj x y` via `Finset.mem_biUnion` + `mem_image` + `mem_filter` + `mem_innerVertexBoundary_iff` + `Prod.mk.injEq`), `edgeBoundary_empty` (`∂^e ∅ = ∅` from `innerVertexBoundary_empty`), `edgeBoundary_card_le_sum_degrees_innerVertexBoundary` (`(∂^e S).card ≤ ∑ x ∈ ∂_i^v S, G.degree x` via `Finset.card_biUnion_le` + `card_image_le` + `card_filter_le` + `card_neighborFinset_eq_degree`). ℤ^d wrapper `latticeGraph_edgeBoundary_card_le_two_mul_d_mul_innerVertexBoundary_card`: `((latticeGraph d).∂^e S).card ≤ 2d · ((latticeGraph d).∂_i^v S).card`. Together with PRs #785 / #786 this closes the elementary boundary-primitive infrastructure (outer/inner vertex + oriented edge) for any locally finite simple graph; subsequent Simon-Lieb work (Epic #780 B-iii) is research-level. (PR #787.) |
| §5.1 | Edge boundary card disjoint biUnion equality (≤ → =) | **Done (PR #787 bound strengthened to equality)** | `SimpleGraph.edgeBoundary_card_eq_sum_inner_filter` (`Concrete/LatticeGraphBED.lean`): `(edgeBoundary G S).card = ∑ x ∈ innerVertexBoundary G S, ((neighborFinset x).filter (· ∉ S)).card`. The `biUnion` defining `edgeBoundary` is disjoint across `x ∈ ∂_i^v S` (different first coordinates), so `Finset.card_biUnion` applies; each summand's image cardinality equals the filter's via `Finset.card_image_of_injective` (`y ↦ (x, y)` injective in `y`). Bounding each filter cardinality by `(neighborFinset x).card = G.degree x` recovers PR #787's inequality `edgeBoundary_card_le_sum_degrees_innerVertexBoundary`. (PR #788.) |
| §5.1 | Edge boundary card outer-side equality (companion of PR #788) | **Done (double-counting identity completed)** | `SimpleGraph.edgeBoundary_card_eq_sum_outer_filter` (`Concrete/LatticeGraphBED.lean`): `(edgeBoundary G S).card = ∑ y ∈ outerVertexBoundary G S, ((neighborFinset y).filter (· ∈ S)).card` — counts crossing edges by their outside endpoint. Proof: rewrite `edgeBoundary G S` as a disjoint `biUnion` indexed by `y ∈ ∂_o^v S` via `Finset.ext` (forward / backward use `mem_edgeBoundary_iff`, `mem_outerVertexBoundary_iff`, and `G.symm` to swap adjacency), then `Finset.card_biUnion` (disjointness across distinct second coordinates) + `Finset.card_image_of_injective` (`x ↦ (x, y)` injective in `x`). Combined with PR #788's inner-side equality this yields the double-counting identity `∑ x ∈ ∂_i^v S, |N(x) \ S| = ∑ y ∈ ∂_o^v S, |N(y) ∩ S|`. (PR #789.) |
| §5.1 | Vertex-by-edge boundary bounds (`|∂_i^v|, |∂_o^v| ≤ |∂^e|`) | **Done (boundary calculus rounded out)** | `SimpleGraph.innerVertexBoundary_card_le_edgeBoundary_card` and `SimpleGraph.outerVertexBoundary_card_le_edgeBoundary_card` (`Concrete/LatticeGraphBED.lean`). Each `x ∈ ∂_i^v S` has a witness neighbour `y ∉ S` from `mem_innerVertexBoundary_iff`, so the filter `(N(x)).filter (· ∉ S)` has cardinality `≥ 1`; sum monotonicity from PR #788's `edgeBoundary_card_eq_sum_inner_filter` then gives `|∂_i^v S| ≤ |∂^e S|`. Symmetric proof for the outer-side bound via `mem_outerVertexBoundary_iff`, `G.symm`, and PR #789's `edgeBoundary_card_eq_sum_outer_filter`. Together with the existing edge-boundary bounds these give the linear chain `|∂_i^v|, |∂_o^v| ≤ |∂^e| ≤ deg_max · |∂_i^v|` (on `latticeGraph d` the rightmost is `2d · |∂_i^v|`, PR #787 + #784). (PR #790.) |
| §5.1 | Edge boundary outer-side degree bound + ℤ^d wrapper (companion of PR #787) | **Done (boundary calculus made symmetric)** | `SimpleGraph.edgeBoundary_card_le_sum_degrees_outerVertexBoundary`: `(edgeBoundary G S).card ≤ ∑ y ∈ outerVertexBoundary G S, G.degree y` (`Concrete/LatticeGraphBED.lean`), via PR #789's `edgeBoundary_card_eq_sum_outer_filter` + per-summand `Finset.card_filter_le` + `card_neighborFinset_eq_degree`. ℤ^d wrapper `latticeGraph_edgeBoundary_card_le_two_mul_d_mul_outerVertexBoundary_card`: `((latticeGraph d).edgeBoundary S).card ≤ 2d · ((latticeGraph d).outerVertexBoundary S).card`, combining the generic outer-side bound with PR #784's `latticeGraph_degree_le`. Together with the inner-side bound (PR #787) gives the strictly stronger combined bound `|∂^e S| ≤ 2d · min(|∂_i^v S|, |∂_o^v S|)` on `latticeGraph d`. (PR #791.) |
| §5.1 | **Random current foundation (Simon-Lieb attempt step 1, Issue #780 multi-PR start)** | **Foundation (Current type + Zero/Add)** | `IsingModel.Ambient.Current G Λ` (`RandomCurrent.lean`): `(inducedGraph G Λ).edgeSet → ℕ`, the type of ℕ-valued currents on a finite induced subgraph used in the random-current representation of the Ising 2-point function (FV §3.10.6, pp. 143–145). Instances: `Current.instZero` (constant zero), `Current.instAdd` (pointwise addition). First PR in the §5.1 Simon-Lieb multi-PR series; subsequent PRs will add parity at vertex, source/sink characterisation, the switching lemma, and the random-current representation `⟨σ_A⟩^Λ = (current sum) / Z`. (PR #796.) |
| §5.1 | **Random current expression of the Ising correlation function (unconditional capstone) + N → ∞ limit chain + switching prep** (multi-PR series, Issue #780) | **Done (20 PRs: Layer 4 LHS+RHS limit, Layer 5 monotone-convergence + uniform bound + unconditional capstone, switching prep primitives)** | **Capstone (PR #865, unconditional)**: `CurrentBounded.pow_card_mul_iSup_weightSum_eq_sum_spinA_prod_exp` (`RandomCurrent.lean`): for `0 ≤ β J`, `2^|Λ| · (⨆_N CurrentBounded.weightSum N A β J) = ∑_σ σ_A · ∏_e Real.exp (β J σ_e)` — the random-current expression of the Ising correlation function `Z · ⟨σ_A⟩` (FV §3.10.6, p. 144) **without any external `Summable`/`BddAbove` hypothesis**. **Layer 4 (LHS+RHS N→∞ limit, PRs #850-#859)**: `Real.exp_eq_tsum_div_factorial`, `Real.tendsto_partial_sum_atTop_exp`, `Config.tendsto_prod_Fin_partial_sum_atTop_prod_exp`, `_sum_spinA_prod_partial_sum_..._sum_spinA_prod_exp`, `_pow_card_mul_currentBounded_weightSum_atTop_sum_spinA_prod_exp` (LHS capstone); `Current.boundedFinset` + `mem_boundedFinset_iff` + `boundedFinset_mono` + `exists_mem_boundedFinset` (cofinality) + `tendsto_boundedFinset_atTop_finsetAtTop` + `Summable.tendsto_sum_boundedFinset` + `CurrentBounded.weightSum_eq_sum_boundedFinset` + `CurrentBounded.tendsto_weightSum_atTop_currentWeightSum` (RHS capstone, summable hyp). **Layer 5 (monotone-convergence + uniform bound + unconditional, PRs #860-#865)**: `CurrentBounded.weightSum_mono` (under `0 ≤ β J`) + `_tendsto_weightSum_atTop_iSup` (BddAbove + `tendsto_atTop_ciSup`) + `Real.partial_sum_le_exp_of_nonneg` (per-edge bound) + `CurrentBounded.weightSum_le_exp_pow_card` (uniform N-independent bound `≤ exp(βJ)^|edgeSet|`) + `_tendsto_weightSum_atTop_iSup_of_nonneg` (unconditional, BddAbove discharged). **Switching prep (PRs #866-#867)**: `Current.le_def` (Pi LE pointwise) + `Current.zero_le` + `Current.le_self_add_right`/`_left` + `Current.subFinset n` (Finset of `m ≤ n` via `Fintype.piFinset (Finset.range (n e + 1))`) + `Current.mem_subFinset_iff` + `Current.subFinset_card_eq_prod` (`= ∏ e, (n e + 1)`); the parameterizing set for the Aizenman pair-bijection `{(n₁, n₂) : n₁ + n₂ = n} ↔ {m : m ≤ n}` (Aizenman 1982 Lemma 3.2, p. 7 / FV §3.10.6, p. 145). Truncation: `Current.instSub` (Pi pointwise via Nat.sub) + `Current.sub_apply` + `Current.sub_add_cancel_of_le` (`(n-m)+m = n`, name aligns with `Nat.sub_add_cancel`) + `Current.add_sub_cancel_of_le` (commuted: `m+(n-m) = n`) + `Current.sub_le_self` (n-m ≤ n). Pair-Finset (PR #868): `Current.pairFinset n : Finset (Current G Λ × Current G Λ)` (= `(subFinset n).image (m ↦ (m, n - m))`) + `mem_pairFinset_iff` (`(m₁, m₂) ∈ pairFinset n ↔ m₁ + m₂ = n`) + `pairFinset_card_eq_subFinset_card` (image of injective fn) + `pairFinset_card_eq_prod` (`= ∏ e, (n e + 1)`); the LHS Finset of the Aizenman switching pair-bijection. Pair-weight scaling (PR #869): `Current.sum_pairFinset_eq_sum_subFinset` (`∑ p ∈ pairFinset n, f p = ∑ m ∈ subFinset n, f (m, n - m)`, sum reindexing via `Finset.sum_image`) + `Current.sum_pairFinset_weight_mul_weight` (`∑ p ∈ pairFinset n, weight β J p.1 * weight β J p.2 = weight β J n * ∑ m ∈ subFinset n, jointFactor m (n - m)`, the central scaling identity combining sum reindexing + `weight_mul_weight_eq_weight_add_mul_jointFactor` (PR #845) + `add_sub_cancel_of_le` (PR #867)). Sub source-set algebra (PR #870): `Current.sub_sources_eq_symmDiff` (`m ≤ n → (n - m).sources = symmDiff (sources n) (sources m)`, via `sub_add_cancel_of_le` + `add_sources_eq` + symmDiff involution) + `Current.sub_hasSources_iff` (`m ≤ n → (n - m).HasSources A ↔ symmDiff sources_n sources_m = A`). Sub trivial algebra (PR #871): `Current.sub_zero` (`n - 0 = n`) + `Current.zero_sub` (`0 - n = 0`, truncated) + `Current.sub_self` (`n - n = 0`) + `Current.subFinset_zero` (`subFinset 0 = {0}`). Pair-Finset boundary cases (PR #872): `Current.zero_mem_pairFinset` (`(0, n) ∈ pairFinset n`) + `Current.self_mem_pairFinset` (`(n, 0) ∈ pairFinset n`) + `Current.pairFinset_zero` (`pairFinset 0 = {(0, 0)}`). Sub_sub_self + compl involution (PR #873): `Current.sub_sub_self_of_le` (`m ≤ n → n - (n - m) = m`) + `Current.subFinset_image_compl` (`(subFinset n).image (m ↦ n - m) = subFinset n`); the natural involution on subFinset induced by pair swap. Pair swap + nonempty (PR #874): `Current.pairFinset_image_swap_eq_self` ((pairFinset n).image Prod.swap = pairFinset n, by add_comm) + `Current.pairFinset_nonempty` ((pairFinset n).Nonempty since (n, 0) ∈ pairFinset n). JointFactor closed form (PR #875): `Current.jointFactor_compl_eq_prod_choose` (m ≤ n → jointFactor m (n - m) = ∏ e, C(n e, m e)) + `Current.sum_subFinset_jointFactor_compl_eq_pow_two` (∑ m ∈ subFinset n, jointFactor m (n - m) = 2^(∑ e, n e), via `Finset.prod_univ_sum` Fubini + `Nat.sum_range_choose` (binomial row sum) + `Finset.prod_pow_eq_pow_sum`). Pair-weight closed form capstone (PR #876): `Current.sum_pairFinset_weight_mul_weight_eq_weight_pow_two` (`∑ p ∈ pairFinset n, weight β J p.1 * weight β J p.2 = weight β J n * 2^(∑ e, n e)`, combining PR #869 + PR #875). Source-conditioned pair Finset (PR #877): `Current.pairFinset_with_sources n A B` (filter of `pairFinset n` by `n₁.HasSources A ∧ n₂.HasSources B`) + `mem_pairFinset_with_sources_iff` + `pairFinset_with_sources_eq_empty_of_sources_mismatch` (when symmDiff A B ≠ sources n). Source-conditioned subFinset (PR #878): `Current.subFinset_with_source n A` (filter of `subFinset n` by `m.HasSources A`) + `mem_subFinset_with_source_iff` + `subFinset_with_source_subset`; dual to pairFinset_with_sources via pair-bijection. Pair ↔ subFinset bridge (PR #879): `Current.pairFinset_with_sources_eq_image_subFinset_with_source` (when `symmDiff sources_n A = B`, pairFinset_with_sources n A B = (subFinset_with_source n A).image (m ↦ (m, n - m))) + `Current.sum_pairFinset_with_sources_eq_sum_subFinset_with_source` (sum reindexing). Source-conditioned pair-weight identity (PR #880, GJ-proposition unit): Main theorem: `Current.sum_pairFinset_with_sources_weight_mul_weight` (under `symmDiff sources_n A = B`, `∑ pairFinset_with_sources, weight n_1 * weight n_2 = weight n * ∑ subFinset_with_source, jointFactor m (n - m)`) + Corollary: `_of_mismatch` (sum = 0 when `symmDiff A B ≠ sources n`); the source-conditioned analog of PR #876. Source-conditioned Finset zero boundary cases (PR #881, GJ-proposition unit, 4 lemmas): `Current.subFinset_with_source_zero` (= {0}) + `Current.subFinset_with_source_zero_of_nonempty` (= ∅ for A ≠ ∅) + `Current.pairFinset_with_sources_zero` (= {(0,0)}) + `Current.pairFinset_with_sources_zero_of_nonempty` (= ∅ if A ≠ ∅ or B ≠ ∅). Source-conditioned weight bound (PR #882, GJ-proposition unit): Main theorem: `Current.sum_subFinset_with_source_jointFactor_le_pow_two` (`∑ subFinset_with_source, jointFactor m (n - m) ≤ 2^(∑ e, n e)`, filter ≤ unrestricted via PR #875) + Corollary: `Current.sum_pairFinset_with_sources_weight_mul_weight_le` (under `0 ≤ β J` + `symmDiff sources_n A = B`, pair-weight ≤ `weight n * 2^(∑ e, n e)`). Source-conditioned pair swap symmetry (PR #883, GJ-proposition unit, 3 lemmas): `Current.pairFinset_with_sources_image_swap_eq` ((pairFinset_with_sources n A B).image Prod.swap = pairFinset_with_sources n B A) + `Current.sum_pairFinset_with_sources_image_swap_eq` (sum invariance) + `Current.pairFinset_with_sources_card_eq_swap` (card symmetric in A, B). Current.support algebra completion (PR #884, GJ-proposition unit, 3 lemmas): `Current.mem_support_iff` (e ∈ support ↔ n e ≠ 0) + `Current.support_sub_subset` ((n - m).support ⊆ n.support) + `Current.support_eq_empty_iff` (support = ∅ ↔ n = 0). Current adjacency from support (PR #885, GJ-proposition unit, 4 lemmas): `Current.Adj n u v` (vertices distinct + edge in support contains both) + `Current.Adj_irrefl` + `Current.Adj_symm` + `Current.Adj_of_zero_iff` (= False); foundation for switching lemma connectivity arguments. The original-plan `Current.summable_weight` (Pi-type Fubini for tsum, → `Current.weightSum = ⨆ N, CurrentBounded.weightSum`) is deferred — monotone-convergence route via uniform bound suffices for the unconditional capstone. **Simon-Lieb prep — connectivity + peeling (PRs #888-#898, all under Issue #780)**: `Current.sources_card_even` (|∂n| ∈ 2ℤ, PR #894 step 98) + `Current.instDecidableAdj` + `mem_toSimpleGraph_support_of_mem_sources` (PR #895 step 99) + `Current.sum_parity_closed_eq_zero` (local handshake in closed Finset) + `sources_reachable_of_sources_eq_pair` (∂n = {i,j} → i,j connected in toSimpleGraph, PR #896 step 100) + `Current.weight_pred_edge` (exact peeling `w(n) = β*J/n_e * w(n-1_e)`) + `weight_le_mul_pred_edge` (bound `w(n) ≤ β*J * w(n-1_e)`, PR #897 step 101) + `Current.sources_sub_edge_symmDiff` (bridge: (n-1_e).sources = symmDiff n.sources endpoints(e)) + `Current.weightSum_pair_le_edge_sum` (edge-peeling bound: weightSum {i,j} ≤ β*J * ∑_{e ∋ i} weightSum (symmDiff {i,j} endpoints(e)); full proof via Finset.map+Finset.sum_map+Summable.sum_le_tsum, PR #898 step 102). Switching Lemma (PR #899 step 103): `Current.subFinset_with_source_card_switching` (|subFinset_with_source n A| = |subFinset_with_source n B| when ∂n=A△B, bijection m↦n-m via `Finset.card_nbij'`) + `Current.sum_subFinset_with_source_weight_mul_weight_switching` (∑ w(m)*w(n-m) invariant under m↦n-m, via `Finset.sum_nbij'`); the Aizenman switching lemma (FV Lemma 3.55, p. 144 / Aizenman 1982). Simon-Lieb inequality (PR #900, step 104, GJ-proposition unit, Inequalities/SimonLieb.lean):  (weightSum = ⨆N weightSum_N, RandomCurrent.lean) +  (bridge identity) +  (boltzmannWeight at h=0 = ∏_e exp(βJ spinEdgeProduct σ e)) +  (correlation A = weightSum A / weightSum ∅) +  (⟨σ_iσ_j⟩ ≤ βJ⋅∑_{e∋i} ⟨σ^{{i,j}△e}⟩; Simon 1980; Lieb 1980). **The Simon-Lieb inequality is now formally proved.** High-temperature susceptibility bound (PR #901, step 105, GJ-proposition unit, Inequalities/SimonLieb.lean): `correlation_sum_le_of_high_temp` — for `0 ≤ βJ`, max degree `D`, `βJD < 1`: `∑_{j≠i} ⟨σ_iσ_j⟩ ≤ βJD/(1-βJD)` (Simon 1980; Lieb 1980; FV §3.7.3). Proof: iterate Simon-Lieb + fixed-point argument (T_k ≤ βJD(1+M) for all k; M ≤ βJD/(1-βJD)). Helpers: `symmDiff_pair_pair_of_ne` ({i,j}△{i,u}={u,j}) + `correlation_inducedGraph_nonneg` (≥ 0 via weightSum ratio). Infinite-volume susceptibility bound (PR #902, step 106, GJ-proposition unit, Inequalities/HighTemp.lean): `susceptibilityΛ_le_of_high_temp` + `susceptibilityAlongExhaustion_le_of_high_temp` + `susceptibilityInfinite_le_of_high_temp` + `susceptibilityInfinite_latticeGraph_le_of_high_temp` — χ_∞(i) ≤ βJD/(1-βJD) at h=0, βJD<1; for ℤ^d, D=2d. Private helper `edgeFilter_card_eq_degree` connects filter.card to `G.degree v` via incidenceFinset bijection. High-temperature cluster property (PR #903, steps 107-108, GJ-proposition unit, Inequalities/HighTemp.lean): `truncated2Infinite_summable_of_high_temp` (Ferromagnetic `⟨J,0,β⟩` + βJD<1 → j↦U₂(i,j) summable, via `summable_of_sum_le` + partial-sum bound through `le_of_tendsto` + `tendsto_finset_sum` + `sum_correlationAlongExhaustion_le_susceptibilityAlongExhaustion`); `clusterProperty_of_high_temp` (cluster property via `clusterProperty_of_summable`); `clusterProperty_latticeGraph_of_high_temp` (ℤ^d, D=2d). **High-temperature cluster property is now formally proved.** (PRs #850-#903, all under Issue #780.) |

ℤ^d wrappers (`Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/CorrelationDecay.lean`): `clusterProperty_latticeGraph_of_summable`, `_J_zero`, `_beta_zero`. **Polynomial-decay corollary** (`TheoremEtaLe1.lean`): `clusterProperty_latticeGraph_of_polynomialDecay` — if `HasPolynomialDecay d Λ p`, then clusterProperty holds (GJ §5.1/§17.8 corollary, Step 140). | §17.8 / §5.1 | **UNCONDITIONAL high-temperature discharge of the polynomial-decay hypothesis** (Issue #4273, audit B6) | **Done (`HasPolynomialDecay` promoted hypothesis→theorem at `βJ·2d < 1`; §17.8 η≤1 + §5.1 cluster property as unconditional corollaries)** | `Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/TheoremEtaLe1/MainTheorem.lean`. GJ Theorem 17.8.1 (p. 316) *assumes* polynomial decay `lim ⟨σ₀σ_x⟩^∞·\|x\|^{d−1} = 0` (hypothesis (17.8.1) = `HasPolynomialDecay`) as the scaling hypothesis; the conditional theorems above are faithful to the book. This row **discharges that hypothesis at high temperature**, where it is provable. **`hasPolynomialDecay_of_hasExponentialDecay`** — at `h = 0`, any positive-rate exponential decay of the truncated two-point function forces `HasPolynomialDecay`: the envelope `C·exp(−α·dist)·dist^{d−1} → 0` (exp beats any power, `tendsto_const_mul_exp_neg_mul_pow_atTop` from Mathlib's `Real.tendsto_pow_mul_exp_neg_atTop_nhds_zero`) squeezes the signed `⟨σ₀σ_x⟩^∞·dist^{d−1}` to `0` along cofinite (`truncated2Infinite_h_zero` + `tendsto_latticeDistance_atTop_cofinite` on the punctured lattice). **`hasPolynomialDecay_latticeGraph_of_betaJ_two_d_lt_one`** — composes the unconditional high-temperature exponential decay `hasExponentialDecay_latticeGraph_of_betaJ_two_d_lt_one` (Simon–Lieb peeling, rate `−log(βJ·2d) > 0`) with the bridge, giving `HasPolynomialDecay d (cubicExhaustion d) ⟨J,0,β⟩` unconditionally at `0<J`, `0<β`, `βJ·2d < 1`. **`correlationInfinite_exponential_of_betaJ_two_d_lt_one`** (§17.8 η≤1) and **`clusterProperty_latticeGraph_of_betaJ_two_d_lt_one`** (§5.1 cluster property) — the resulting unconditional high-temperature capstones, feeding the discharged hypothesis into the conditional theorems. (Discharging only the high-temperature slice; at/below criticality polynomial decay is genuinely the scaling hypothesis, matching GJ p. 77 §5.2.) **Honesty note:** the genuinely-new content is `hasPolynomialDecay_latticeGraph_of_betaJ_two_d_lt_one` (the hypothesis becomes a theorem); the §17.8 η≤1 capstone is logically a round-trip (high-temp exp decay → weaken to polynomial → §17.8 re-derives exp), and the §5.1 cluster property was *already* unconditional at high temperature via the independent summability route `clusterProperty_latticeGraph_of_high_temp` (#903, `SummabilityCluster.lean`) — this is the alternative §17.8-discharge route through the GJ-named theorem. Axiom-free (`#print axioms` = `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]`; no Vitali). References: Glimm–Jaffe, 2nd ed., §17.8 pp. 316–318, §5.1; FV §3.7.3. |
| §17.5 | **∞-volume Simon-Lieb inequality (Step 109, Issue #780)** | **Done** | `correlationInfinite_simon_lieb` + `correlationInfinite_simon_lieb_latticeGraph` (`Inequalities/HighTemp.lean`): for `h = 0`, `0 ≤ βJ`, `i ≠ j`, and `¬G.Adj i j` (i not adjacent to j), `⟨σ_iσ_j⟩_∞ ≤ βJ · ∑_{k~i} ⟨σ_kσ_j⟩_∞`. The hypothesis `¬G.Adj i j` excludes the self-correlation issue: the finite-vol Simon-Lieb edge {i,j} (when i~j) gives symmDiff = ∅ → correlation ∅ = 1, but `{j,j} = {j}` as a Finset so `correlationInfinite p {j,j} = magnetization = 0` at h=0. Proof: `ciSup_le` reduces to per-stage bounds; subset case applies `correlation_inducedGraph_simon_lieb`; the edge filter sum maps injectively into `G.neighborFinset i` via `Sym2.Mem.other` (injectivity from `Sym2.other_spec`); each term bounded by `correlationAlongExhaustion_le_correlationInfinite`; sum bound via `Finset.sum_le_sum_of_subset_of_nonneg`. Three private helpers: `symmDiff_pair_pair_of_ne'` (local copy), `correlationInfinite_nonneg_of_hβJ` (nonnegativity from `hβJ` only). ℤ^d wrapper: direct application of the abstract theorem. Reference: Simon 1980, Comm. Math. Phys. 77, 111–126; Lieb 1980, Comm. Math. Phys. 77, 127–135. (Issue #780.) |
| §17.5 | **High-temperature exponential decay (Step 110, Issue #780)** | **Done** | `hasExponentialDecay_of_high_temp` (`Concrete/LatticeGraphCorrelation/LatticeMassHighTemperature.lean`): for `0 ≤ βJ` and `βJD < 1` (D = 2d), `HasExponentialDecay d (cubicExhaustion d) ⟨J,0,β⟩ (-log(βJD))` with witness `C = 1/(1-βJD)`. Key private inductive lemma `correlationInfinite_latticeGraph_le_of_dist_ge`: for `dist(i,j) ≥ n+1`, `⟨σ_iσ_j⟩_∞ ≤ (βJD)^n · (βJD/(1-βJD))`. Base case n=0: per-stage one-term ≤ sum comparison (nonneg truncated2 via random-current representation) + susceptibility bound (Step 106). Inductive step: `dist ≥ n+2 → ¬Adj → Simon-Lieb (Step 109) + triangle inequality + IH on each neighbor`. Main theorem: set n = dist-1, convert `(βJD)^dist` to `exp(-(-log βJD) * dist)` via `Real.exp_log` / `Real.log_pow`; edge case βJD=0 handled separately (Lean's `Real.log 0 = 0` gives trivial rate 0; physically, βJD=0 means J=0 or β=0 so the bound holds trivially). Auxiliary additions to `Inequalities/HighTemp.lean`: `susceptibilityAlongExhaustion_latticeGraph_le_of_high_temp` (per-stage ℤ^d susceptibility bound) + `correlationInfinite_nonneg_of_hβJ` made public. Reference: Simon 1980, Comm. Math. Phys. 77, 111–126; Lieb 1980, Comm. Math. Phys. 77, 127–135. (Issue #780, PR #916.) |
